## Chapter 18 (Cluster expansion)

|---|---|---|---|
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent high-temperature closed form (first brick in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `partitionFunction_high_temp_expansion_field_closed` + helper `sum_prod_toSign_pow_field` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/HighTempClosed/ClosedFormField.lean`): finite-volume closed form `Z(G;J,h,β) = 2^{\|ι\|} · cosh(βJ)^{\|E\|} · cosh(βh)^{\|ι\|} · ∑_{X⊆E} tanh(βJ)^{\|X\|} · tanh(βh)^{\#\text{odd-deg}(X)}`, generalizing Friedli–Velenik §3.7.3 eq. (3.45) (zero-field case) by field-dependent single-site Fubini factors `cosh(βh)^{\text{even}} · sinh(βh)^{\text{odd}}`. Purely finite combinatorial identity; no cluster-expansion machinery or new analytic input. First brick of the project complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain. Reference: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 and its answer on p. 531 give the exact general-field closed weight; §3.7.3, eqs. (3.41)–(3.45), pp. 116–117, give the h = 0 template. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent polymer factorization identity (brick 2a in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `allSubgraphs_sum_eq_vdConnectedPolymerFamilies_sum` + `fieldPolymerWeight` + `oddCard_biUnion_of_vd` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/Families/FieldConnectedPolymers.lean`): field-dependent polymer factorization identity `∑_{X⊆E} tanh(βJ)^{|X|}·tanh(βh)^{#odd(X)} = ∑_{Γ∈vdConnectedPolymerFamilies G} ∏_{P∈Γ} w(P)`, where `w(P) = tanh(βJ)^{|P|}·tanh(βh)^{#odd(P)}`. Factorization of the first-brick closed form over the edge-connected components (polymers) of `X`, exhibiting the finite-volume partition function as a hard-core (vertex-disjoint) polymer gas with field-dependent activity. The key new ingredient is the odd-degree vertex-count additivity `oddCard_biUnion_of_vd`, generalizing the even-degree restriction of the zero-field analogue `evenSubgraphs_sum_eq_vdPolymerFamilies_sum` (`Families/VertexDisjoint.lean`). Purely finite combinatorial identity; no Kotecký–Preiss machinery or convergence content (bricks 2b onward). Second brick of the project complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain. Reference: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight, §§5.2–5.3 and §5.6 give the abstract hard-core polymer formalism, and §3.7.3, eq. (3.45), p. 117 gives the h = 0 template. The field-dependent connected-component factorization is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent partition function as a polymer gas (brick 2a capstone) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `partitionFunction_high_temp_expansion_field_polymer_family` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/HighTempClosed/ClosedFormFieldPolymer.lean`): substituting the factorization identity into the first-brick closed form leaves `Z(G;J,h,β)` written as `2^{\|ι\|}·cosh(βJ)^{\|E\|}·cosh(βh)^{\|ι\|}` times a sum over vertex-disjoint connected polymer families of `∏_P fieldPolymerWeight (βJ) (βh) P`. This is the form the Kotecký–Preiss machinery is stated over — a hard-core polymer gas whose activity carries the field only through the odd-degree exponent — so it is what makes the general-field case reachable by the same route as the zero-field one, on the same bounded-degree graph and with no maximum-degree blow-up. Purely finite and combinatorial: no activity bound, convergence claim or `h`-analyticity is asserted here. Reference: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §3.7.3, eq. (3.45), p. 117 (`h = 0` template) and §5.7; Glimm–Jaffe §18.4 is the continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent per-vertex / total / per-site polymer activity bounds (brick 2b in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `sum_pow_card_le_geometric_of_count_le` + `fieldRootedPolymerActivity_le_geometric` + `fieldAllPolymersActivity_le_card_mul_geometric` + `fieldAllPolymersActivity_div_card_le_geometric` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldPolymerActivity.lean`): volume-uniform geometric bounds for the field-dependent polymer activities from brick 2a. Under the high-temperature window `Δ²·|tanh a| < 1` (`Δ = G.maxDegree`, `= 2d` on the lattice), the per-vertex field activity sum is `∑_{P ∋ v} |w_{a,b}(P)| ≤ (1 − Δ²|tanh a|)⁻¹`, the total activity is `≤ |ι|·(1 − Δ²|tanh a|)⁻¹`, and per-site is the constant `(1 − Δ²|tanh a|)⁻¹`, uniformly in volume and field. The field factor `|tanh(b)|^{#odd(P)} ≤ 1` (real `b`, via `Real.abs_tanh_lt_one`) always helps the bound; **no field hypothesis on `b`**. The bounds are **never** literal `< 1` (always `≥ 1`), hence a volume-uniform finiteness input for Kotecský–Preiss, **not** the KP smallness criterion (which is the later field Mayer bound). Real `h` only; complex `h` (where `|tanh b|` need not be `≤ 1`) is implemented in the later row. Generic helper `sum_pow_card_le_geometric_of_count_le` factors out the counting core for the field-dependent connected polymers; the pre-existing zero-field even version stays untouched (does not use the helper). Third brick of the project complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain. Reference: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §3.7.3, Lemma 3.38 and eqs. (3.48)–(3.49), give the h = 0 counting templates, Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight, and §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 gives the convergence criterion. The stated field-dependent per-vertex, total, and per-site activity bounds are project extensions. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent Mayer term and its dominated summability (brick 3 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldClusterSeqActivity` + `fieldMayerExpansionTerm` + `abs_fieldClusterSeqActivity_le` + `summable_fieldMayerExpansionTerm` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldMayerTerm.lean`): the convergence input for the field-dependent Mayer expansion under the high-temperature window `e·(∑_{P ∈ allConnectedPolymers G} |tanh a|^{|P|}) < 1`. **Brick 3 is pure comparison**: the field cluster-sequence activity `fieldClusterSeqActivity a b ω = ∏ᵢ w_{a,b}(ω i)` (multiplicative carry of the field weight, analogous to `clusterSeqActivity t ω` for the zero-field case) is term-by-term dominated by the zero-field activity `clusterSeqActivity |tanh a| ω` via `abs_fieldClusterSeqActivity_le`. The field Mayer term `fieldMayerExpansionTerm G n a b` sums the weight-agnostic Ursell coefficient `ursellCoefficient` (reused verbatim from the zero-field route) over cluster sequences in the connected species `allConnectedPolymers G` (brick 2a's parity-unrestricted polymers), weighted by `fieldClusterSeqActivity`. Summability `summable_fieldMayerExpansionTerm` is discharged by the already-proven species- and weight-agnostic spanning-tree majorant `Penrose.summable_completeGraph_numSpanningTrees_div_factorial_mul_pow` (used verbatim from `MayerCore/UrsellMajorant.lean`), collapsed onto the zero-field series via the term-by-term domination — **no new hard analysis, purely a comparison argument**. Real `h` only (complex `h` is handled by the later non-vanishing and local-limit rows); regression at `b = 0` recovers the zero-field even restriction. Brick 3 of the project complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain. Reference: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §§5.2–5.4 and Proposition 5.3 give the abstract polymer/Ursell and convergence framework, while Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight. The field-dependent dominated-summability comparison is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field-dependent Mayer-Montroll identity (brick 4 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldPolymerZ` + `fieldPolymerFreeEnergy` + `fieldColorDegreeTerm` + `field_mayer_identity_general` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldMayerIdentity.lean`): the algebraic field-dependent Mayer–Montroll identity `fieldPolymerFreeEnergy G a b = ∑' n, fieldMayerExpansionTerm G n a b`, stating that the field polymer free energy equals the field Mayer series. The identity itself is a combinatorial Fubini swap of the colour-degree double sum (reusing the weight-agnostic Ursell coefficient, incompatibility graph, and proper-surjective-colouring counts from the zero-field `h = 0` route, `MayerCore/MayerMontroll.lean` PR #3998) with the field-dependent cluster-sequence activity `fieldClusterSeqActivity a b ω = ∏ᵢ w_{a,b}(ω i)` carrying through identically. Convergence is imported from brick 3 (domination `|fieldClusterSeqActivity a b ω| ≤ clusterSeqActivity |tanh a| ω`). Real `h` only; complex `h` (where `|tanh b|` need not be `≤ 1`) is implemented in the later row. Regression at `b = 0` (`tanh 0 = 0`) collapses the field weight to zero-field monomials and recovers the zero-field even-restricted identity. This is supporting infrastructure in the completed complex-field local-limit chain; it does not itself discharge the wider real infinite-volume derivative contract. A complementary `field_mayer_identity_general_eventually` supplies the frequency-domain version: for fixed `b`, in some neighbourhood of `a = 0`, the identity holds as both convergence hypotheses (`|ε_{a,b}| < 1` and `e·A_C(a) < 1`) hold eventually. Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §§5.2–5.4 and Proposition 5.3 give the abstract polymer/Ursell and log-Ξ formalism, while Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight. The field-dependent Mayer–Montroll identity is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. (Issue #4413, thread #4433.) |
| §17.6.1 | Field polymer `exp` identity, real-`h` non-vanishing, and complex-`h` prelude (brick 5 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldPolymerZ_pos` + `exp_fieldPolymerFreeEnergy` + `fieldPolymerZ_ne_zero` + `fieldPolymerZ_eq_exp_tsum_fieldMayerExpansionTerm` (real `h`); `fieldPolymerWeightℂ` + `fieldPolymerZℂ` (defs); `fieldPolymerWeightℂ_ofReal` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_ofReal` (real-axis agreement); `oddBoundary_card_le_two_mul_card` + `norm_fieldPolymerWeightℂ_le` + `norm_fieldPolymerZℂ_le` (complex estimates) (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldPolymerExpNonvanishing.lean`): Brick 5 converts brick 4's Mayer–Montroll **log**-identity into an **exponential** identity and the associated **non-vanishing** of the field polymer partition function. At real `h`: under `|ε_{a,b}| < 1`, the partition function `fieldPolymerZ G a b = 1 + ε_{a,b} > 0` (positivity via `ε > -1`); then `Real.exp (fieldPolymerFreeEnergy) = fieldPolymerZ` (via `Real.exp_log`); hence `fieldPolymerZ ≠ 0` (`exp`-image). The four reals are: `fieldPolymerZ_pos` (positivity), `exp_fieldPolymerFreeEnergy` (exp identity), `fieldPolymerZ_ne_zero` (non-vanishing, phrased via `exp` to mirror the complex brick 6), and `fieldPolymerZ_eq_exp_tsum_fieldMayerExpansionTerm` (partition function as `exp` of the Mayer series). Complex prelude (field `b ∈ ℂ`, coupling `a ∈ ℝ`): type design of the complex field weight `w^ℂ_{a,b}(P) = (tanh a : ℂ)^{|P|} · (Complex.tanh b)^{#odd(P)}` with `fieldPolymerWeightℂ` and the partition-function wrapper `fieldPolymerZℂ`; real-axis agreement `fieldPolymerWeightℂ_ofReal` and `fieldPolymerZℂ_ofReal`; the universal parity bound `oddBoundary_card_le_two_mul_card : #odd(P) ≤ 2·|P|` (edge set covers ≤ 2·|P| odd-degree vertices); and the `M²`-inflated majorant `norm_fieldPolymerWeightℂ_le` and `norm_fieldPolymerZℂ_le` (with `M = max(1, ‖Complex.tanh b‖)`) that carries the complex non-vanishing used by the later assembly. Honest scope: brick 5 supplies the real-`h` `exp`/non-vanishing and the complex **type/estimate design only**; the complex `Z_ℂ ≠ 0` body is brick 6. **Not itself a real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt` result**; this is part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §§5.2–5.5 and Proposition 5.3 give the abstract polymer/Ursell, convergence, and analytic context, while Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight. The real/complex exponential identities and non-vanishing package are project extensions. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Complex field partition `exp` identity and non-vanishing via analytic continuation (brick 6 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldClusterSeqActivityℂ` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ` + `fieldClusterSeqActivityℂ_ofReal` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_ofReal` (complex Mayer term and real-axis agreement); `norm_fieldClusterSeqActivityℂ_le` + `norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_le_tree_activity_pow` (Penrose majorant bound); `cosh_ne_zero_of_norm_lt_pi_div_two` + `differentiableOn_ctanh_ball` + `analyticOnNhd_ctanh_ball` (pole avoidance and `Complex.tanh` analyticity on `‖b‖ < π/2` ball); `fieldPolymerWeightℂ_analyticAt` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_analyticOnNhd` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_analyticOnNhd` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_tsum_analyticOnNhd` (LHS and RHS holomorphy); `fieldPolymerZ_eq_exp_eventually_b` (real-axis seed in `h`-direction); `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_exp_tsum_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ` (complex `exp` identity via analytic continuation on the π/2-ball); `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero` (complex non-vanishing) (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldPolymerComplexNonvanishing.lean`): Brick 6 delivers the **complex analytic continuation** of the field polymer partition function `Z_C(b)`, the complex non-vanishing, and the associated exponential identity on a complex `b`-ball `‖b‖ < π/2`. It is the complex-analytic body, lifting the real-axis identity (brick 5) to the complex plane. **Complex inputs**: the complex Mayer term `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ G n a b = ∑_{ω ∈ connected} (ursellCoefficient ω : ℂ) · ∏ᵢ w^ℂ_{a,b}(ω_i)` (brick 5's complex weight cast to `ℂ`); the `M²`-inflated Penrose majorant at the activity `Mr² · |tanh a|` (where `M = max(1, ‖Complex.tanh b‖)`, `Mr ≥ 1` is the uniform ball bound); the **pole-avoidance lemma** `‖b‖ < π/2 ⟹ Complex.cosh b ≠ 0` (new to mathlib: `cosh` zeros are at `(2k+1)·π/2`, so a zero would need `‖b‖ ≥ π/2`), ensuring `Complex.tanh = sinh/cosh` is analytic on the ball; analyticity of both sides (partition function `Z_C` and the RHS `exp(∑_n fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ)`) via Weierstrass (`Complex.differentiableOn_tsum_of_summable_norm` + dominated convergence of the Penrose majorant) and finite composition of analytic weights. **Analytic continuation**: a tail of the real sequence `b_k = 1/(k+1) → 0` lies in the open ball, and brick 5's seed `fieldPolymerZ_eq_exp_eventually_b` (cast through `Complex.ofReal_exp`) yields equality frequently with interior limit point `0`; by the **identity theorem** `AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_frequently_eq` (two analytic functions on a preconnected open set that agree frequently along a sequence accumulating at an interior point agree everywhere), the identity lifts to the whole ball. **Non-vanishing**: the `exp` identity and `Complex.exp_ne_zero` immediately give `fieldPolymerZℂ G a b ≠ 0` for all `b` in the ball. Honest scope: brick 6 delivers only the complex `exp` identity and `≠ 0`; the two-point Montel local bounds are brick 7, the F6 holomorphic local-limit endpoint is brick 8. **Not itself a real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt` result**; this is part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 supplies parameter-analyticity context, §§5.2–5.4 and Proposition 5.3 supply the prior abstract polymer/Ursell and convergence roles, and Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight. The complex identity-theorem continuation and non-vanishing result are project extensions. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Volume-uniform per-order geometric bound and summability for complex field Mayer term (bricks F1a–F1b in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | **F1a**: `connectedPolymerGasData` + `polymerSupport_card_le_two_mul_of_mem_allConnectedPolymers` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_norm_le_treeSum_activity` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_norm_le_treeSum_rootedExpActivity` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric`. **F1b**: `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_zero` + `summable_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_of_tail_condition` + `summable_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_of_tail_condition` + `tsum_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_le` + `tsum_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_div_card_le` + `tsum_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_div_card_le` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldMayerTermPerOrderBound.lean`): Bricks F1a–F1b of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. The field partition function is a hard-core gas of the *connected* field polymers `allConnectedPolymers G`, with activity `w^ℂ_{a,b}(P) = (tanh a)^{|P|}·(tanh_ℂ b)^{#odd(P)}` and inflated coupling `t_∗ = (max 1 ‖Complex.tanh b‖)²·|tanh a|`. **F1a supplies three ingredients**: **(i)** the connected gas as an abstract `PolymerGasData` instance (`connectedPolymerGasData`, discharging the three fields from `mem_allConnectedPolymers`); **(ii)** the support hypothesis at `c = 2` (`polymerSupport_card_le_two_mul_of_mem_allConnectedPolymers`: `|supp P| ≤ 2·|P|` for connected polymers); **(iii)** the field-Mayer → Penrose-tree bridge (`fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_norm_le_treeSum_activity` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_norm_le_treeSum_rootedExpActivity`). Feeding these into the abstract peel/geometric-closing chain (R1–R3 refactor), the headline per-order geometric bound is `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric`: `‖fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ G (n+1) a b‖ ≤ |ι|/(1−r_∗)·(8 r_∗/(1−r_∗)²)ⁿ`, with `r_∗ = Δ²·e·t_∗` and `Δ = G.maxDegree`, volume-uniform per site (dividing by `|ι|` leaves a constant depending only on `Δ`, `a`, and the field ball radius through `M`). **F1b extends F1a with summability**: the geometric majorant is summable (ratio `ρ_∗ = 8 r_∗/(1−r_∗)² < 1`), yielding `Summable` for both the shifted (`n ≥ 1`) and full (`n ≥ 0`) series; the `n = 0` term vanishes (`fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_zero`); explicit bounds on the partial sums (`tsum_norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_succ_le` and per-site variant) enable the volume-uniform (per-site intensive) bound on the full sum `‖∑'_n‖ / |ι| ≤ const`. Supporting implementation (not themselves real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt` result); part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik, §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 gives the lattice-polymer convergence criterion, §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 supplies parameter-analyticity context, and Kotecký–Preiss, Comm. Math. Phys. **103** (1986) 491–498, Theorem 1 is the original criterion. The explicit per-order geometric/tree-majorant package is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6.1, pp. 313–314 is the motivating theorem, and Chapter 18, pp. 321–343 is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Coupling-complexified holomorphic field Mayer tower (brick F2-pre in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_summable_norm` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_tsum_analyticOnNhd` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_tanh_eq` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldMayerCouplingTower.lean`): Brick F2-pre of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. The real-coupling family `{a ↦ fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ G n a b}` (F1a–F1b) needs extension to a genuine holomorphic-in-coupling tower on an open complex neighbourhood for the real-`a` identity theorem (brick F2). This file builds the **coupling-complexified** field Mayer term `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling G n τ b` by replacing `τ = (Real.tanh a : ℂ)` with a free complex variable `τ ∈ ℂ` (field `b ∈ ℂ` stays fixed), yielding polymer weights `w_{τ,b}(P) = τ^{|P|}·(Complex.tanh b)^{#odd(P)}`. **F2-pre supplies five ingredients**: **(i)** the definition `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling`; **(ii)** the `‖τ‖`-geometric per-order bound `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric` (mirror of F1a with `|tanh a| ↦ ‖τ‖`), volume-uniform per site; **(iii)** ball summability `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_summable_norm` (mirror of F1b); **(iv)** holomorphy of the `τ`-`tsum` on `Metric.ball 0 ρ` via `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_tsum_analyticOnNhd` (Weierstrass: each term entire, geometric majorant uniform on the ball); **(v)** substitution bridge `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂCoupling_tanh_eq` connecting back to F1's real-coupling term. **Scope**: Supporting implementation (not itself a real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt` result); prerequisite for the real-`a` identity theorem F2. The `τ`-`tsum` is holomorphic on a ball `‖τ‖ < ρ` (volume-uniform, per-site intensive), **not** a volume-decay result. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik, Exercise 5.8 (general-field weight), §§5.2–5.3 and Proposition 5.3 (abstract polymer/Ursell and log-Ξ framework), §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 (convergence), and §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 (parameter analyticity), together with Kotecký–Preiss, Comm. Math. Phys. **103** (1986) 491–498, Theorem 1. The coupling-complexified holomorphic tower is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6.1, pp. 313–314, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Degree-window field `exp` identity and non-vanishing via real-`a` analytic continuation (brick F2 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_exp_tsum_of_degree_window` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero_of_degree_window` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldExpIdentityDegreeWindow.lean`): Brick F2 upgrades brick 6's complex field `exp` identity from the *extensive* window (volume-dependent, shrinking as `|ι| → ∞`) to the *degree window* `(W_Δ)` — a condition on `(Δ = G.maxDegree, a, R)` only, **volume-uniform** — via *analytic continuation in the real coupling `a`* on the preconnected interval `[0, A)`. The four continuation steps: **(Step 0)** `analyticOnNhd_ctanh_ofReal` establishes that `a ↦ Complex.tanh(↑a)` is `ℝ`-analytic on all of `ℝ` (using `ofReal` ℝ-analyticity + `Complex.tanh` holomorphy off `cosh`-zeros + `cosh(↑a) ≠ 0`). **(F2a)** the RHS `∑' n, fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ G n a b` is `AnalyticOnNhd ℝ` in `a` on `[0, A)` by composing F2-pre's `τ`-holomorphy with Step 0. **(F2b)** the small-`a` seed holds (brick 6 applies near `0`). **(F2c)** `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_exp_tsum_of_degree_window` spreads the identity across `[0, A)` via the identity theorem: LHS is a finite polynomial in `Complex.tanh(↑a)` hence ℝ-analytic, RHS is the `exp` composition (F2a), they agree near `0` (F2b), so on the preconnected open interval both branches must agree everywhere. **(F2d)** `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero_of_degree_window` is immediate from the identity and `Complex.exp_ne_zero`. **Scope**: Supporting implementation (not itself a real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt` result); this is the degree-window (volume-uniform) `exp` identity + non-vanishing — the essential input for the complex-field local-limit endpoint and the subsequent Vitali–Porter bounded-analytic-function machinery. This is part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik, Exercise 5.8 (general-field weight), §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 (convergence), and §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 (parameter analyticity), together with Kotecký–Preiss (1986), Theorem 1. The identity-continuation and non-vanishing argument on the degree window is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6.1, pp. 313–314, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Volume-uniform avoiding-gas ratio exp bound (brick F3 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `IsConnectedPolymer_Gavoid_iff` + `allConnectedPolymers_Gavoid` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_sub_Gavoid_eq_touching_sum` + `norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_sub_Gavoid_le` + `field_gavoid_degree_window` + `norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_tsum_sub_Gavoid_le_support_card` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_Gavoid_div_norm_le_exp` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldAvoidingRatio.lean`): Brick F3 of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. This is the field/connected mirror of the `β`-only avoiding-ratio unit. For a set `C` of edges, `Gavoid G C = G.deleteEdges (touchEdges G C)` is `G` with every edge touching `polymerSupport C` deleted. With `M = max 1 ‖Complex.tanh b‖`, the inflated per-site activity is `t_∗ = M²·|tanh a|`, `r_∗ = Δ²·e·t_∗` (`Δ = G.maxDegree`), and the local Kotecký–Preiss constant is `κ_Δ = (1−r_∗)⁻¹·(1−ρ_∗)⁻²` with the connected-gas ratio `ρ_∗ = 8 r_∗/(1−r_∗)²` (support constant `c = 2`). **The headline is the volume-uniform ratio bound**: `‖Zᶠ(Gavoid G C)/Zᶠ(G)‖ ≤ exp(κ_Δ·|polymerSupport C|)`, where `Zᶠ(G) = fieldPolymerZℂ G a b`. **κ_Δ depends only on Δ, t_∗, c = 2, NOT on the volume |ι|**, which allows the ratio bound to survive the infinite-volume limit and feed the completed local-limit assembly. This F3 brick does not itself export a real derivative. The downstream `IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationInfiniteFieldDeriv.lean` derives real differentiability only for normalized `⟨a,b,1⟩`, small `a`, and `0 < b < r < π/2`; `b = 0`, the full nonperturbative range, and a derivative-series identity, sign, or uniform bound remain outside that capstone. Both partitions are non-vanishing on the window (immediate from F2's degree-window `exp` identities), so the ratio is a genuine quotient. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight and §§5.2–5.4 give the abstract polymer/Ursell and convergence context. The ratio-exponential and avoiding-ratio assembly is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6.1, pp. 313–314 is the motivating theorem. |
| §17.6.1 | General-boundary field two-point closed form (brick F4a in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `oddFilter_add_indicator_eq_symmDiff` + `sum_spinProduct_edgeSpin_field_closed` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/CorrelationClosed/GeneralFieldClosed.lean`): Brick F4a of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. The field two-point correlation ratio on a finite volume Λ factors over edge-connected polymers (via brick 2a's polymer factorization); the inner σ-sum over the spin configuration is then evaluated **in closed form** for a general observable A (set of vertices) and geometric boundary ∂X (set of edges touching X). The key combinatorial facts: **(i)** parity symmetry of the field term yields a symmetric difference `∂X △ A` of the field-weighted boundary (`oddFilter_add_indicator_eq_symmDiff`); **(ii)** the closed form of the inner σ-sum factors as the field polymer partition `fieldPolymerZ` denominator (`sum_spinProduct_edgeSpin_field_closed`). Result: general A and geometric boundary ∂X enter symmetrically via the soft field weight `tanh(βh)^{|∂X △ A|}`, generalizing the Friedli–Velenik high-temperature formula (pair case A = {x, y}) to arbitrary observables. Pure parity machinery; no cluster-expansion or Kotecký–Preiss inputs. Purely finite combinatorial identity, closing the pair-only gap of the zero-field route where isolated source X = ∅ broke symmetry. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the exact general-field weight and Exercise 3.23 gives the h = 0 two-point template; eq. (3.46) is an h = 0 one-point formula and is not a source for this exact statement. The arbitrary-A/general-h closed form is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, supplies only the continuum P(φ)₂ analogy, not a lattice two-point source. |
| §17.6.1 | Complexified field two-point numerator and local analyticity (brick F4b-1 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldTwoPointNumℂ` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_ofReal` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_analyticOnNhd` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/CorrelationClosed/GeneralFieldClosedComplex.lean`): Brick F4b-1 of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. The field two-point correlation numerator is lifted from real `h` to complex `b ∈ ℂ` via the complex field weight `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G a b x y A = ∑_{σ} (field weight σ) · (spinProduct σ A)`, where `a ∈ ℝ` (coupling) and `b ∈ ℂ` (field). The primary ingredients: **(i)** definition `fieldTwoPointNumℂ` extends the real numerator (F4a) by substituting `Complex.tanh b` for `Real.tanh h`; **(ii)** real-axis agreement via `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_ofReal` ensures continuity with F4a (numerator in real `h` recovers by substitution `b ↦ ↑h`); **(iii)** local holomorphy `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_analyticOnNhd` establishes analyticity on `Metric.ball 0 r` with `r ≤ π/2` (where `Complex.tanh` has no poles, since `cosh b = 0` only at `(2k+1)·π i/2`). The complex denominator `fieldPolymerZℂ` is already analytic (brick 6's result), so the numerator's analyticity feeds the subsequent complex-field ratio and local-limit machinery. **Scope**: This brick supplies only the complex numerator and its local analyticity; the complex denominator identification and the complex two-point ratio bound are supplied by the subsequent F4b rows. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the general-field weight, Exercise 3.23 gives the h = 0 two-point template, and §5.5 supplies parameter-analyticity context; eq. (3.46) is h = 0 and one-point only. The complex numerator and ball holomorphy are project extensions. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, supplies only the continuum P(φ)₂ analogy, not a lattice analyticity source. |
| §17.6.1 | Complex all-subgraphs ↔ families denominator bridge (brick F4b-2a in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `fieldPolymerWeightℂ_biUnion_of_vd` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_allSubgraphs_sumℂ` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_empty_eq_fieldPolymerZℂ` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/CorrelationClosed/GeneralFieldClosedComplex.lean`): Brick F4b-2a of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. The complex denominator of the field two-point ratio is the `A = ∅` numerator `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G ∅ a b` (since `∂X △ ∅ = ∂X`); this brick bridges it to the complex field polymer partition function `fieldPolymerZℂ`, so that F2's non-vanishing `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero` (Kotecký–Preiss window) transports to the denominator. **(i)** `fieldPolymerWeightℂ_biUnion_of_vd` is the complex mirror of the real weight factorization `fieldPolymerWeight_biUnion_of_vd` over a vertex-disjoint family (`Finset.card_biUnion` + `oddCard_biUnion_of_vd`, both ring-independent). **(ii)** `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_allSubgraphs_sumℂ` re-runs the real bridge's `Finset.sum_bij` (`X ↔ polymerDecomposition X`) **over ℂ**: the membership/injectivity/surjectivity obligations are weight-free (reusing the real named lemmas), only the terminal ring identity moves `ℝ → ℂ`. A `push_cast` of the real bridge would hold only on the real `b`-axis, whereas the ratio analyticity on the `π/2`-ball needs the identity at every complex `b`. **(iii)** capstone `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_empty_eq_fieldPolymerZℂ` identifies `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G ∅ a b = fieldPolymerZℂ G a b` via `∂X △ ∅ = ∂X`. **Scope**: this brick supplies only the denominator bridge; the complex-`h` correlation ratio object `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G A a b / fieldTwoPointNumℂ G ∅ a b` and its ball analyticity (`AnalyticAt.div`) are supplied by the subsequent F4b-2b row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4459). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight, while §3.7.3, eq. (3.45), p. 117 is an h = 0 template only. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, supplies only the continuum P(φ)₂ analogy, not a lattice cluster-expansion source. |
| §17.6.1 | Complex-`h` field correlation ratio object + local analyticity (brick F4b-2b in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldCorrelationℂ` + `fieldCorrelationℂ_ofReal` + `fieldCorrelationℂ_ofReal_eq_correlation` + `fieldCorrelationℂ_analyticOnNhd` (`IsingModel/Conditioning/CorrelationClosed/GeneralFieldClosedComplex.lean`): Brick F4b-2b of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. Assembles the complex-`h` field correlation ratio object and its holomorphy from the F4b-1/F4b-2a ingredients. **(i)** `fieldCorrelationℂ G A a b = fieldTwoPointNumℂ G A a b / fieldTwoPointNumℂ G ∅ a b` is the complex numerator over its empty-observable specialization (the denominator taken as `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G ∅`, not the families form, so numerator and denominator share the same `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_ofReal` cast). **(ii)** `fieldCorrelationℂ_ofReal` gives real-axis agreement with the honest F4a ratio, **unconditionally** (via `Complex.ofReal_div`, needing no denominator non-vanishing); `fieldCorrelationℂ_ofReal_eq_correlation` then connects it to the physical correlation `correlation G p A` at real Ising parameters `a = β·J`, `b = ↑(β·h)` through `correlation_high_temp_expansion_general_h_closed`. **(iii)** `fieldCorrelationℂ_analyticOnNhd` establishes `AnalyticOnNhd ℂ` on `Metric.ball 0 r` with `r ≤ π/2`, **conditional** on the abstract hypothesis `hden : ∀ w ∈ ball 0 r, fieldPolymerZℂ G a w ≠ 0`: numerator and denominator are analytic (`fieldTwoPointNumℂ_analyticOnNhd`), and `hden` transports to the denominator through the F4b-2a bridge `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_empty_eq_fieldPolymerZℂ`, so `AnalyticAt.div` applies pointwise. **Scope**: `hden` is intended to be discharged by the **volume-uniform** `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero_of_degree_window` (Δ-based Kotecký–Preiss window; the volume-dependent `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero` is *not* used, since the F6 Vitali/Montel infinite-volume consumer requires uniformity). | §17.6.1 | Complex field numerator source/avoid weight factorization (brick F5-pre-1 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `fieldSourceWeightℂ` + `fieldSourceWeightℂ_union_avoiding` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_fieldSourceWeightℂ` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourceAvoidFactor.lean`): Brick F5-pre-1 of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. Records the purely combinatorial weight factorization underlying the F5 source peel, which splits each subgraph `X` of the complex field numerator `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G A a b = ∑_{X ⊆ E}(tanh a : ℂ)^{|X|}·(tanh_ℂ b)^{|∂X △ A|}` into a part `S` carrying the observable `A` and an `A`-avoiding remainder `Y`. **(i)** `fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b S = (tanh a : ℂ)^{|S|}·(tanh_ℂ b)^{|∂S △ A|}` is the `A`-marked per-source summand (`∂S = oddBoundary S`, `△ = symmDiff`); it is `fieldPolymerWeightℂ` with `∂S` replaced by `∂S △ A`, so `A = ∅` recovers `fieldPolymerWeightℂ` (`fieldSourceWeightℂ_empty_eq_fieldPolymerWeightℂ`; `fieldSourceWeightℂ_empty_source` gives the `S = ∅` observable factor `(tanh_ℂ b)^{|A|}`). **(ii)** `fieldSourceWeightℂ_union_avoiding`: for a vertex-disjoint union `X = S ∪ Y` with `Y` avoiding `A` (`Disjoint (polymerSupport Y) A`), `fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b (S ∪ Y) = fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b S · fieldPolymerWeightℂ a b Y`. The `tanh(a)^{|·|}` factor uses edge-disjoint cardinality additivity (`IsPolymerVertexDisjoint.toEdgeDisjoint`); the `(tanh b)^{|·|}` factor uses `oddBoundary_union_of_vertexDisjoint` (`∂(S ∪ Y) = ∂S ∪ ∂Y`) and the core boundary-cardinality identity `symmDiff_card_union_of_disjoint` (`Q` disjoint from `P` and `A` ⟹ `|(P ∪ Q) △ A| = |P △ A| + |Q|`, proved by direct `sdiff` expansion, no `Finset.card_symmDiff` dependency; `∂Y` disjoint from `∂S` via `oddBoundary_disjoint_of_vertexDisjoint`, from `A` via `oddBoundary_subset_polymerSupport` + avoidance). **(iii)** `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_fieldSourceWeightℂ` rewrites the numerator as `∑_{X ⊆ E} fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b X` (definitional), the connection point consumed by the F5 source peel. **Scope**: this brick supplies only the binary touch/avoid weight factorization (purely `Finset` symmDiff/cardinality bookkeeping, no new analysis). The complete source peel bijection (fixing the source index / `Gavoid` deletion target) is brick F5-pre-2, and the **volume-uniform geometric ratio bound** (true new mathematics: F3 avoiding-block cancellation + geometric decay) is F5; both implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4461). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the exact field weight, while §3.7.3, eq. (3.47), and Exercise 3.23 give h = 0 peel templates. The arbitrary-A complex source/avoid factorization is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6, Theorem 17.6.1, p. 313, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only. |
| §17.6.1 | Vertex-set avoiding-graph field partition ratio exp bound (brick F5-pre-2a in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `GavoidVertex` + `Gavoid_eq_GavoidVertex_polymerSupport` + `maxDegree_GavoidVertex_le` + `IsConnectedPolymer_GavoidVertex_iff` + `allConnectedPolymers_GavoidVertex` + `fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_sub_GavoidVertex_eq_touching_sum` + `field_gavoidVertex_degree_window` + `norm_fieldMayerExpansionTermℂ_tsum_sub_GavoidVertex_le_card` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_GavoidVertex_div_norm_le_exp` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldVertexAvoidingRatio.lean`): Brick F5-pre-2a of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. **Vertex-set generalization of brick F3** (`FieldAvoidingRatio.lean`): F3 deletes every edge touching `polymerSupport C` for an edge set `C`, whereas the F5 source peel must delete every edge touching an **arbitrary vertex set** `W = polymerSupport S ∪ A` (the `A`-collar), which is in general not a polymer support (the observable `A` may be isolated / non-adjacent). Hence `GavoidVertex G W = G.deleteEdges (touchVertexEdges G W)` (delete every edge touching `W`) is unavoidable; the bridge `Gavoid_eq_GavoidVertex_polymerSupport` records `Gavoid G C = GavoidVertex G (polymerSupport C)` (definitional). The delete-edges machinery (connected-polymer characterizations, Mayer-difference touching decomposition, `c = 2` degree-window transfer via `maxDegree_GavoidVertex_le`) is re-derived over the raw vertex set `W` (mirror of `AvoidingDeleteEdges.lean` + `FieldAvoidingRatio.lean` with `polymerSupport C` replaced by `W`; merged F3 files untouched). **The headline is the volume-uniform ratio bound** `‖Zᶠ(GavoidVertex G W)/Zᶠ(G)‖ ≤ exp(κ_Δ·W.card)`, where `Zᶠ(G) = fieldPolymerZℂ G a b`, `M = max 1 ‖Complex.tanh b‖`, `t_∗ = M²·|tanh a|`, `r_∗ = Δ²·e·t_∗` (`Δ = G.maxDegree`), `κ_Δ = (1−r_∗)⁻¹(1−ρ_∗)⁻²`, `ρ_∗ = 8 r_∗/(1−r_∗)²`. The exponent `W.card` is the general-vertex-set analogue of F3's `|polymerSupport C|`, and **κ_Δ depends only on Δ, t_∗, c = 2, NOT on the volume |ι|**. Both partitions are non-vanishing on the degree window (F2). **Scope**: this brick supplies only the vertex-set avoiding-graph ratio bound (infrastructure); the source peel bijection (`fieldSourceConfigs G A` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_source_avoid`) is brick F5-pre-2b, and the true new mathematics (per-source bound + geometric aggregation → volume-uniform two-point ratio bound) is brick F5a; both implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4462). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. (3.47), gives the h = 0 peel template. The degree-uniform vertex-deletion ratio is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6, Theorem 17.6.1, p. 313, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only. |
| §17.6.1 | Complex field numerator source peel bijection (brick F5-pre-2b in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `sourcePartOf` + `avoidPartOf` + `polymerDecomposition_union_of_vertexDisjoint` (crux H1) + `fieldSourceConfigs` + `fieldSourcePairs` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_fieldSourcePairs` + `avoidingSum_eq_fieldPolymerZℂ_GavoidVertex` + `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_source_avoid` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourcePeel.lean`): Brick F5-pre-2b of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint. Combines the F5-pre-1 weight factorization (`fieldSourceWeightℂ_union_avoiding`) with the F5-pre-2a vertex-set avoiding graph (`GavoidVertex`, `subset_edgeFinset_GavoidVertex_iff`) into the **source peel identity**: each subgraph `X` of the numerator `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G A a b = ∑_{X ⊆ E} fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b X` splits along its polymer decomposition into the `A`-touching source part `sourcePartOf A X` (the **full union** of `A`-touching components — no unique anchor, unlike the `β`-route `htSubgraphSum_anchored_peel_component`) and the `A`-avoiding remainder `avoidPartOf A X`. **The map `X ↦ (sourcePartOf A X, avoidPartOf A X)` is a bijection onto `fieldSourcePairs G A`** (pairs `(S, Y)` with `S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A` a source config — every component touches `A` —, `Y ⊆ E` vertex-disjoint from `S` and avoiding `A`), inverse `(S, Y) ↦ S ∪ Y`, via `Finset.sum_bij'` (5 obligations mirroring the `β` peel). The inverse is well defined by the **crux helper H1** `polymerDecomposition_union_of_vertexDisjoint` (`IsPolymerVertexDisjoint S Y ⟹ polymerDecomposition (S ∪ Y) = polymerDecomposition S ∪ polymerDecomposition Y`, elementary, local to this PR); the forward-map weight factors by `fieldSourceWeightℂ_union_avoiding`. Reducing the inner `A`-avoiding sum at fixed `S` — index set `{Y : S ⊥ Y ∧ Y ⊥ A}` identified with `(GavoidVertex G (polymerSupport S ∪ A)).edgeFinset.powerset` via `subset_edgeFinset_GavoidVertex_iff` + `fieldPolymerZℂ_eq_allSubgraphs_sumℂ` — gives the **capstone** `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_source_avoid`: `fieldTwoPointNumℂ G A a b = ∑_{S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A} fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b S · fieldPolymerZℂ (GavoidVertex G (polymerSupport S ∪ A)) a b`. The `A`-collar `W = polymerSupport S ∪ A` matches F5-pre-2a's vertex set exactly; the empty source `S = ∅` recovers the isolated observable term. Structural safeguard: the `A`-avoiding remainder's double-counting of `A` is excluded by the collar `GavoidVertex G (polymerSupport S ∪ A)`. **Scope**: purely combinatorial (no numerical estimate); the per-source norm bound + source counting + geometric aggregation into the volume-uniform correlation bound (the genuine new analysis, math-before-code) is brick F5a, implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4463). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. | §17.6.1 | Per-source complex weight geometric bound (brick F5a-1 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `polymerSupport_card_le_two_mul_card` + `fieldSourceWeightℂ_norm_le` (private, ineq. (I)) + `fieldSourceWeightℂ_norm_mul_exp_le` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourceWeightBound.lean`): Brick F5a-1 of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint — the **first genuinely new analytic sub-brick** of the field CE (math-before-code, `.self-local/tex/thm-17-6-1-h-F5a1-per-source-weight-bound.tex`). The F5-pre-2b source peel `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_source_avoid` writes the numerator as `∑_{S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A} w^ℂ_A(S)·Zᶠ(GavoidVertex G (∂S ∪ A))/Zᶠ(G)` (collar `∂S ∪ A = polymerSupport S ∪ A`). This brick bounds the **per-source weight** together with the collar `exp`-factor that the F5-pre-2a ratio bound contributes, in a form **parametric in the aggregation exponent `κ`** so F5a-3 can instantiate `κ := κ_Δ` decoupled from the F5a-2 fiber count. **(I)** `fieldSourceWeightℂ_norm_le`: with `Mr = max(1, ‖Complex.tanh b‖)` (`hMr1 : 1 ≤ Mr`, `hMr : ‖Complex.tanh b‖ ≤ Mr`), `‖fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b S‖ ≤ Mr^{|A|}·(Mr²·|tanh a|)^{|S|}` — four-step: factor the norm (`Complex.norm_real` gives `‖(tanh a : ℂ)‖ = |tanh a|`), bound the field base by `Mr` (`pow_le_pow_left₀`, base monotone), `|∂S △ A| ≤ 2|S| + |A|` via inclusion `∂S △ A ⊆ ∂S ∪ A` (`Finset.symmDiff_subset_union`, avoiding the subtractive `Finset.card_symmDiff`) + `Finset.card_union_le` + `oddBoundary_card_le_two_mul_card`, inflate the `Mr`-exponent using `1 ≤ Mr` (`pow_le_pow_right₀`, exponent monotone) and split `Mr^{2|S|+|A|} = Mr^{|A|}·(Mr²)^{|S|}` (`pow_add`, `pow_mul`). **(II)** `polymerSupport_card_le_two_mul_card`: universal parity bound `|polymerSupport P| ≤ 2·|P|` (no membership hypothesis; collar analogue of `oddBoundary_card_le_two_mul_card`), supplying `|∂S ∪ A| ≤ 2|S| + |A|`. **capstone** `fieldSourceWeightℂ_norm_mul_exp_le` (`hMr1`, `hMr`, `hκ : 0 ≤ κ`): `‖fieldSourceWeightℂ A a b S‖·exp(κ·|∂S ∪ A|) ≤ Mr^{|A|}·exp(κ·|A|)·(Mr²·e^{2κ}·|tanh a|)^{|S|}`, multiplying (I) by the collar `exp`-bound `exp(κ·|∂S ∪ A|) ≤ exp(κ·|A|)·(e^{2κ})^{|S|}` (`Real.exp_le_exp`, `Real.exp_nat_mul`, `Real.exp_add`) and regrouping the two `|S|`-powers (`mul_pow`) into the geometric ratio base `a₀ = Mr²·e^{2κ}·|tanh a|`. **Scope**: this is the self-contained per-source *activity* estimate (field-CE analogue of the GJ §18.3 polymer activity bound); the volume-independent source-config fiber count `|{S : |S|=ℓ}| ≤ (c_A·Δ²)^ℓ` is brick F5a-2 and the geometric aggregation `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` is brick F5a-3, both implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4464). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. | §17.6.1 | Antidiagonal-tuple cardinality arithmetic bound (brick F5a-2a in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `antidiagonalTuple_card_le` (`IsingModel/Combinatorics/AntidiagonalTupleCard.lean`): Brick F5a-2a of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint — the purely arithmetic composition ("stars and bars") factor of the volume-independent source-config fiber count F5a-2. Records `(Finset.Nat.antidiagonalTuple k ℓ).card ≤ (ℓ + 1) ^ k`: an element of `Finset.Nat.antidiagonalTuple k ℓ` is a tuple `t : Fin k → ℕ` with `∑ i, t i = ℓ`, so each coordinate `t i ≤ ℓ` (`Finset.single_le_sum`, all terms `≥ 0`), i.e. `t i ∈ Finset.range (ℓ + 1)`; hence the tuple set embeds into `Fintype.piFinset (fun _ : Fin k => Finset.range (ℓ + 1))` (`Finset.card_le_card` + `Fintype.mem_piFinset` + `Finset.mem_range`), whose cardinality is `(ℓ + 1) ^ k` (`Fintype.card_piFinset_const` + `Finset.card_range`). Self-contained (mathlib only), reusable. **Scope**: this is only the arithmetic count of `ℓ`-compositions into `k = |A|` parts; the structural component→`A`-root injection giving the source-config fiber count `|{S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A : |S| = ℓ}| ≤ (2^{|A|}·Δ²)^ℓ` (`fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le`) is brick F5a-2b, and the geometric aggregation `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` is brick F5a-3, both implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4465). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: the antidiagonal-tuple arithmetic is self-contained project combinatorics and hence a project extension; Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), §3.7.3, Lemma 3.38, pp. 116–118, is downstream counting context only, not a direct source. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6, Theorem 17.6.1, p. 313, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only. |
| §17.6.1 | Source-configuration root injection (brick F5a-2b-i in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `fieldSourceRoot` + `fieldSourceRoot_mem` + `fieldSourceRoot_injOn` + `polymerDecomposition_card_le_of_fieldSourceConfigs` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourceRoot.lean`): Stage 1 of the volume-independent source-config fiber count F5a-2b in the completed complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/field-ce-F5a-2b-sourceconfig-fibercount.tex` §Stage 1. A source configuration `S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A` (`FieldSourcePeel.lean`) is an edge subset whose every connected component meets the observable vertex set `A`. Since a component may touch `A` at several vertices, each component `C ∈ polymerDecomposition S` is assigned a **single** root vertex `fieldSourceRoot A C` (via `Classical.choice` on `A ∩ polymerSupport C`, `dite`-totalized to a default vertex on the empty branch — never used in the source context), rather than indexing by "the component containing the `i`-th vertex of `A`" (which would multiply-count a component and break the downstream `∑`-card identity). `fieldSourceConfigs_component_inter_nonempty` rephrases the filter condition `¬ Disjoint (polymerSupport C) A` as `(A ∩ polymerSupport C).Nonempty` (`Finset.not_disjoint_iff_nonempty_inter`); `fieldSourceRoot_mem` then gives `fieldSourceRoot A C ∈ A ∩ polymerSupport C`. **capstone** `fieldSourceRoot_injOn`: the root map is injective on `polymerDecomposition S` — distinct components are vertex-disjoint (`polymerDecomposition_pairwise_vertexDisjoint`) and each root lies in its own component's support, so a shared root would sit in both supports, contradicting disjointness (uses **only** vertex-disjointness; no order on `ι`, no uniqueness of `Classical.choose`). Corollary `polymerDecomposition_card_le_of_fieldSourceConfigs`: `(polymerDecomposition S).card ≤ A.card` (`Finset.card_le_card_of_injOn`, image in `A`). **Scope**: Stage 1 only (root assignment + injectivity + component-count bound); the tuple reconstruction / `∑`-card identity is brick F5a-2b-ii, and the per-factor closed-walk count + composition + capstone `fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le` (`|{S : |S|=ℓ}| ≤ (2^{|A|}·Δ²)^ℓ`) is brick F5a-2b-iii, both implemented in the later row; the geometric aggregation `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` is brick F5a-3. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4466). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: this root-injection construction is project combinatorial infrastructure and hence a project extension; Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 3.23 and §3.7.3, eq. (3.47), provide source-component context, while Lemma 3.38 is downstream counting context only. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6, Theorem 17.6.1, p. 313, is the motivating theorem, while Chapter 18, pp. 321–343, is a continuum P(φ)₂ analogy only. |
| §17.6.1 | Source-configuration tuple injection + ∑-card (brick F5a-2b-ii in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `fieldSourceComp` + `fieldSourceComp_biUnion` + `fieldSourceComp_pairwiseDisjoint` + `fieldSourceComp_card_sum` + `fieldSourceConfigs_comp_injOn` + `fieldSourceCardVec` + `fieldSourceCardVec_mem_antidiagonalTuple` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourceRoot.lean`): Stages 2–3 of the volume-independent source-config fiber count F5a-2b in the completed complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/field-ce-F5a-2b-sourceconfig-fibercount.tex` §Stage 2–3. Building on the F5a-2b-i root assignment, indexes the components of `S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A` by `Fin A.card` via the fixed enumeration `A.equivFin.symm`: `fieldSourceComp A S i` is the (unique, `fieldSourceRoot_injOn`) component whose root is the `i`-th vertex of `A` (or `∅`). **crux** `fieldSourceComp_biUnion`: `⋃_{i} fieldSourceComp A S i = S` — each entry `⊆ S` (`fieldSourceComp_subset`), and every edge `f ∈ S` lies in its own component `edgeComponent S f`, whose root determines the index `i := A.equivFin ⟨root, _⟩` (roundtrip `Equiv.symm_apply_apply`) and whose chosen component equals it by `fieldSourceRoot_injOn`. `fieldSourceComp_pairwiseDisjoint`: distinct indices give distinct roots (`A.equivFin.symm` + subtype coe injective) hence distinct, vertex-disjoint (`polymerDecomposition_pairwise_vertexDisjoint`), hence **edge**-disjoint (`IsPolymerVertexDisjoint.toEdgeDisjoint` — required for `Finset.card_biUnion`). `fieldSourceComp_card_sum`: `∑_i |fieldSourceComp A S i| = |S|` (`card_biUnion` + biUnion cover). `fieldSourceConfigs_comp_injOn`: the tuple map is injective on the fixed-`ℓ` fiber (both sides reconstruct to the same biUnion). Stage 3 `fieldSourceCardVec_mem_antidiagonalTuple`: the cardinality vector `i ↦ |fieldSourceComp A S i|` lies in `Finset.Nat.antidiagonalTuple A.card ℓ` when `|S| = ℓ` — the hand-off into the F5a-2a composition count `(ℓ+1)^{|A|}`. The whole `A ≃ Fin |A|` conversion layer is confined to `A.equivFin` (no sum over the finset `A`); the `∅`-source / `m=0` case is handled uniformly. **Scope**: Stages 2–3 (tuple reconstruction + `∑`-card + injectivity + antidiagonal membership); the per-factor closed-walk count + composition + capstone `fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le` (`|{S : |S|=ℓ}| ≤ (2^{|A|}·Δ²)^ℓ`) is brick F5a-2b-iii, implemented in the later row; the geometric aggregation `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` is brick F5a-3. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4467). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. (3.47), provide source-component context, while Lemma 3.38 is downstream counting context only. | §17.6.1 | Source-configuration fiber count capstone (brick F5a-2b-iii in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Implemented, axiom-free** | `rootedComponentFiber` + `mem_rootedComponentFiber` + `rootedComponentFiber_card_le` + `fieldSourceTarget` + `fieldSourceComp_mem_target` + `fieldSourceTarget_card_le` + `fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldSourceCount.lean`): Stages 4–5 (capstone) of the volume-independent source-config fiber count F5a-2b in the completed complex-field holomorphic local-limit chain; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/field-ce-F5a-2b-sourceconfig-fibercount.tex` §Stage 4–5. Completes F5a-2b by counting the fiber via the F5a-2b-ii component tuple. **per-factor** `rootedComponentFiber G z m` collects the size-`m` edge subsets `C ⊆ G.edgeFinset` that are empty (`m = 0` disjunct — essential since `∅` is neither edge-connected nor has `z ∈ polymerSupport ∅`) or edge-connected with `z ∈ polymerSupport C`; `rootedComponentFiber_card_le`: `|rootedComponentFiber G z m| ≤ Δ^{2m}` (`Δ = G.maxDegree`) — `m = 0` fiber `⊆ {∅}` gives `≤ 1 = Δ^0`, `m > 0` injects into the length-`2m` closed walks from `z` (`card_connected_edge_sets_le`, FV Lemma 3.38) counted by `walksFromCount G z (2m) ≤ Δ^{2m}` (`walksFromCount_le_pow`, `G.degree_le_maxDegree`; both branches uniform). **composition** `fieldSourceTarget A ℓ` biUnions over `antidiagonalTuple A.card ℓ` the `piFinset` of per-root fibers; `fieldSourceComp_mem_target`: the F5a-2b-ii component tuple lands in the target (genuine entries edge-connected `isEdgeConnected_of_mem_polymerDecomposition` with root in support `fieldSourceRoot_mem` → `m>0` disjunct, empty entries → `m=0` disjunct); `fieldSourceTarget_card_le`: `≤ (ℓ+1)^{|A|}·Δ^{2ℓ}` (`Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `Fintype.card_piFinset` + `Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum` collapsing `∏_i Δ^{2t_i} = Δ^{2ℓ}` on the antidiagonal + F5a-2a `antidiagonalTuple_card_le`). **capstone** `fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le`: `|{S ∈ fieldSourceConfigs G A : |S| = ℓ}| ≤ (2^{|A|}·Δ²)^ℓ` — `Finset.card_le_card_of_injOn` with the F5a-2b-ii injection `fieldSourceConfigs_comp_injOn` and `fieldSourceComp_mem_target`, then the ℕ arithmetic `(ℓ+1)^{|A|}·Δ^{2ℓ} ≤ (2^{|A|}·Δ²)^ℓ` folding the composition count into the per-`ℓ` base `(2^{|A|})^ℓ` via `ℓ+1 ≤ 2^ℓ` (`Nat.lt_two_pow_self`) and `Δ^{2ℓ} = (Δ²)^ℓ` (`pow_mul`, `mul_pow`), completed in ℕ. Volume-independent (only `Δ = G.maxDegree` and `|A|`). This completes the F5a-2b fiber count; the geometric aggregation `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` (source peel + F5a-1 κ_Δ + F5-pre-2a ratio + this count + geometric closed sum) is brick **F5a-3**, implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4468). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. | §17.6.1 | Volume-uniform complex field correlation bound (brick F5a-3 capstone in the completed complex-field local-limit chain — **completes F5a**) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldCEKappaDelta` + `fieldCorrelationℂ_eq_sum_source_ratio` (private, Stage 0) + `fieldSourceWeight_mul_ratio_norm_le` (private, Stage a) + `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_uniform` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationUniformBound.lean`): geometric-aggregation capstone of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/thm-17-6-1-h-F5a1-per-source-weight-bound.tex` §sec:f5a3. A **pure assembly** of the already-proved F5a bricks (no new analytic content). Constants (all **volume-free**, `Δ = G.maxDegree`, `M = max 1 ‖Complex.tanh b‖`, `κ_Δ = fieldCEKappaDelta G a b` the F5-pre-2a local KP exponent): `A₀ = M^{|A|}·e^{κ_Δ|A|}`, `a₀ = M²·e^{2κ_Δ}·|tanh a|`, `B = 2^{|A|}·Δ²`, `q = a₀·B`. **Stage 0** `fieldCorrelationℂ_eq_sum_source_ratio` (unconditional, algebraic): source peel `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_eq_sum_source_avoid` + empty-source denominator bridge `fieldTwoPointNumℂ_empty_eq_fieldPolymerZℂ` + `Finset.sum_div` + `mul_div_assoc` write `fieldCorrelationℂ G A a b = ∑_{S} w^ℂ_A(S)·(Zᶠ(GavoidVertex G (∂S ∪ A))/Zᶠ(G))` — **no `Zᶠ(G) ≠ 0` hypothesis** (the field convention `x/0=0` makes `Finset.sum_div` unconditional; the avoiding cancellation is absorbed into the per-source ratio). **Stage a** `fieldSourceWeight_mul_ratio_norm_le`: per-source `‖w^ℂ_A(S)‖·‖ratio_S‖ ≤ A₀·a₀^{|S|}` from F5-pre-2a `fieldPolymerZℂ_GavoidVertex_div_norm_le_exp` (ratio `≤ e^{κ_Δ|∂S ∪ A|}` at collar `W = polymerSupport S ∪ A`) and F5a-1 `fieldSourceWeightℂ_norm_mul_exp_le` (with `κ := κ_Δ`, `Mr := M` the **pointwise** value at `b`, distinct from the ball-uniform F5-pre-2a parameter `Mrb`); `0 ≤ κ_Δ` from `D < 1` (degree window) + `sq_nonneg`. **capstone** `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_uniform`: `norm_sum_le` + `norm_mul` + Stage a + F5a-2b fiber count `fieldSourceConfigsOfCard_card_le` (`|{S : |S|=ℓ}| ≤ B^ℓ`, cast ℕ→ℝ) fed to `sum_le_geometric_closed_of_fiber_card_le` under the **independent field window** `hq : q < 1` give `‖fieldCorrelationℂ G A a b‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)`, **volume-uniform** — the field complex-parameter analogue of `twoPointRatio_norm_le_geometric` and the `hbdd` input the Vitali/Montel the F6 assembly uses. The window `hq` (real high-temp `|tanh a|·Δ²<1` inflated by `M²·e^{2κ_Δ}·2^{|A|}`) is a strictly narrower field-specific hypothesis, **not** implied by the degree window `hkp`/`hρwin`. **This completes F5a** (per-source weight + fiber count + geometric aggregation); the exhaustion-uniform wrap with explicit lattice radius (`Δ = 2d`) is brick **F5b** and the F6 holomorphic local-limit endpoint is brick **F6**, both implemented in the later row. | §17.6.1 | Ball-&-volume-uniform complex field correlation bound (brick F5b-1 crux in the completed complex-field local-limit chain) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldCEKappaOfActivity` + `fieldCEActivity` + `fieldCEKappaDelta_eq_kappaOfActivity` + `fieldCEActivity_nonneg` + `fieldCEActivity_monotone_aux` + `rhoStar_mono` + `fieldCEKappaOfActivity_mono` + `fieldCEKappaDelta_le_of_activity_le` (crux, `D`-monotonicity) + `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_ball_uniform` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationBallUniform.lean`): brick F5b-1 of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/thm-17-6-1-h-F5a1-per-source-weight-bound.tex` §F5b-1 (`sec:f5b1`). Upgrades the F5a-3 **pointwise-in-`b`** bound `‖fieldCorrelationℂ‖ ≤ A₀/(1−q)` into one **uniform over the whole `π/2`-ball** in `b`, the `b`-uniform crux needed to feed the Vitali/Montel brick F6. **No new analytic content beyond F5a-3**; the content is monotonicity of the Kotecký–Preiss closed form in the single polymer **activity** scalar `D = Δ²·e·(M²·|tanh a|)` (`fieldCEActivity`, `Δ = G.maxDegree`, `M = max 1 ‖Complex.tanh b‖`), through which both uniformities factor. **crux** `fieldCEKappaDelta_le_of_activity_le` (`D`-monotonicity): if the pointwise activity `fieldCEActivity G a b ≤ D*` (a larger, `b`-free activity in the KP window), then `fieldCEKappaDelta G a b ≤ κ(D*)`, where `κ(D) = fieldCEKappaOfActivity D = (1−D)⁻¹·((1 − 8D/(1−D)²)⁻¹)²` and `fieldCEKappaDelta G a b = κ(fieldCEActivity G a b)` definitionally (`fieldCEKappaDelta_eq_kappaOfActivity`); `κ(D)` is monotone increasing (`fieldCEKappaOfActivity_mono`: both closed-form factors `(1−D)⁻¹` and `((1−ρ*(D))⁻¹)²` are nonnegative & increasing, `ρ*(D) = 8D/(1−D)²` increasing via `rhoStar_mono`, window positivity `1−D>0`, `1−ρ*>0` preserved under `D ≤ D*`). **capstone** `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_ball_uniform` (general `G`, `b`-uniform): with `Mstar = max 1 Mrb` the F5-pre-2a ball-uniform envelope of `‖tanh b‖` and the **strengthened `Mstar`-window** (`hkpstar`/`hρwinstar`/`hqstar` at `Mstar`, strictly stronger than F5a-3's pointwise window), `‖fieldCorrelationℂ G A a b‖ ≤ A₀*/(1−q*)` with `A₀* = Mstar^{|A|}·e^{κ(D*)|A|}`, `q* = Mstar²·e^{2κ(D*)}·|tanh a|·2^{|A|}·Δ²`, `D* = Δ²·e·(Mstar²·|tanh a|)` — a bound **independent of `b`** over the whole ball. Assembly: `fieldCEActivity_monotone_aux` (activity monotone in `M`) + `rhoStar_mono` downcast the `Mstar`-window to F5a-3's pointwise window at `b`; the F5a-3 capstone gives `A₀(b)/(1−q(b))`; the crux gives `κ_Δ(b) ≤ κ(D*)`; const-mono (TeX `prop:const-mono`: numerator ↑, positive denominator ↓, `div_le_div_iff₀`) closes `A₀(b)/(1−q(b)) ≤ A₀*/(1−q*)`. **Pitfall (TeX `rem:f5b1-pitfalls`)**: `Mstar` is the uniform *envelope* of the pointwise `M`, used **only** to bound `D ≤ D*`, and is never substituted into the pointwise `κ_Δ` that already embeds `M` (that would desynchronise the activity from its own KP exponent); window positivity (`1−D*>0`, `1−ρ*(D*)>0`, `1−q*>0`) is load-bearing at the extremum. **Scope**: F5b-1 supplies only the ball-&-volume-uniform bound (the `hbdd` datum); the exhaustion wrap with explicit lattice radius (`Δ = 2d`) is brick **F5b-2** and the F6 holomorphic local-limit endpoint is brick **F6** (conditional), both implemented in the later row. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4470). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. | §17.6.1 | Exhaustion wrap of the complex field correlation bound (brick F5b-2 in the completed complex-field local-limit chain — **completes F5b**) | **Conditional / limited-range (axiom-free)** | `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_ball_uniform_of_degree_le` (degree-bound version, `IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationBallUniform.lean`) + `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion` (def) + `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_norm_le_uniform` (all-stage volume-uniform bound) + `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd` (per-stage holomorphy) (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationAlongExhaustion.lean`): brick F5b-2 (exhaustion wrap) of the completed field cluster-expansion chain supporting the small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local-limit endpoint; math-before-code `.self-local/tex/thm-17-6-1-h-F5a1-per-source-weight-bound.tex` §F5b-2 (`sec:f5b2`). **This completes F5b.** F5b-1 hard-wires the concrete degree `Δ = G.maxDegree` into both the window and the closed constant, but along a lattice exhaustion each induced stage satisfies only `maxDegree ≤ 2d` (not equality, `induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le`), so a direct substitution would leave the bound `n`-dependent and lose volume uniformity. **(b) degree-bound version** `fieldCorrelationℂ_norm_le_ball_uniform_of_degree_le` (the only new content): carries an **external** `Δ : ℕ`, `hΔ : G.maxDegree ≤ Δ`, phrases both window (`hkpstar`/`hρwinstar`/`hqstar`) and RHS with `(Δ:ℝ)²`, and is proved from F5b-1 by reading the single numeric input `(∗) (G.maxDegree:ℝ)² ≤ (Δ:ℝ)²` (`Nat.cast_le` + `pow_le_pow_left₀`) in **two opposite senses** — **window downcast** (`Δ → G.maxDegree`: the larger-argument Δ-window hypotheses `< 1` downcast to the `G.maxDegree`-window that F5b-1 consumes, via `rhoStar_mono`/`fieldCEKappaOfActivity_mono`) and **RHS upcast** (`G.maxDegree → Δ`: the F5b-1 output constant increases in the degree, the `prop:const-mono` `div_le_div_iff₀` crux replayed in the degree variable). Substituting `Δ = G.maxDegree` (`hΔ = le_rfl`) collapses back to F5b-1 verbatim (degeneracy check). **(a) def** `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion G Λ A a b n` — the per-stage complex field correlation on the induced subgraph of `Λ.volume n` (lifted observable `liftFinset A`, `= 0` before engulfment), the field (`b`-varying, `a` fixed) analogue of `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion`. **(c) exhaustion wrap** `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_norm_le_uniform` — for the lattice exhaustion, feeding `Δ = 2d` (`induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le`) into (b) gives, for **every** stage `n` and every `b` in the `π/2`-ball, a single bound independent of both `n` (volume-uniform) and `b` (ball-uniform); the engulfed branch supplies `Nonempty ↑(Λ.volume n)` from `hA : A.Nonempty`, `liftFinset_card` transports `A.card`, and the pre-engulfment branch is `0 ≤` the nonnegative constant. This is exactly the volume-uniform `hbdd` datum for F6. **(d) per-stage holomorphy** `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd` — each stage is `AnalyticOnNhd ℂ` in `b` on the ball, via F4b `fieldCorrelationℂ_analyticOnNhd` on the engulfed branch (or constant `0`), given the per-stage `hden` (conditional, discharged in F6 by `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero_of_degree_window`). (a)/(c)/(d) are pure `β → b` transcriptions of the `β`-route templates (`correlationComplexAlongExhaustion`, `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_two_point_norm_le_uniform`). **Scope**: F5b-2 completes F5b (the ball-&-volume-uniform along-exhaustion bound + per-stage holomorphy); the field-specific Vitali/Montel consumer and uniform `hden` discharge are implemented by F6a–F6c as the small-coupling holomorphic local-limit endpoint; this F5b brick itself exports no real derivative; the downstream `IsingModel/ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationInfiniteFieldDeriv.lean` supplies only the normalized small-coupling result on `0 < b < r < π/2`, leaving `b = 0`, the full range, and the U3/series, sign, and bound questions outside its scope. Supporting implementation (part of the completed complex-field local-limit chain) (Issue #4413, thread #4433, PR #4471). Axiom-free `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Friedli–Velenik (2017 ed.), Exercise 5.8 gives the field weight, §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 gives convergence, and §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 supplies parameter-analyticity context. The degree-bound and exhaustion-wrap construction is a project extension. Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.6, Theorem 17.6.1, eq. (17.6.1), p. 313 is motivation only, not a lattice source. |
| §17.6.1 | Field Vitali plumbing: real-point bridge, pointwise convergence, and conditional Vitali consumer (F6a) | **Conditional / limited-range** | `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_at_real_eq_ofReal`, `..._tendsto_at_real`, and `..._analytic_of_volume_uniform_bound` implement the real-axis identification and the holomorphic locally-uniform limit under explicit non-vanishing and local-bound hypotheses. This row does not assert a real infinite-volume `HasDerivAt`. |
| §17.6.1 | Volume-uniform field partition non-vanishing (F6b) | **Conditional / limited-range (supporting theorem under the degree-ball window)** | `fieldPolymerZℂ_ne_zero_of_degree_le` and `fieldPolymerZℂAlongExhaustion_ne_zero_on_ball_uniform_latticeGraph` discharge the F6a non-vanishing input on the stated degree/ball window. They are support for the local holomorphic limit, not a real infinite-volume derivative theorem. |
| §17.6.1 | Small-coupling complex-field holomorphic local limit (F6c) | **Conditional / limited-range: implemented for `∃ a₀ > 0`, `0 ≤ a < a₀`, and `0 ≤ b₀ < r < π/2`** | `fieldCorrelationInfinite_latticeGraph_analytic_high_temp` exports a `DifferentiableOn ℂ` limit on `Metric.ball 0 r`, locally-uniform convergence of the finite-stage field correlations, and equality with `correlationInfinite` at the real point `b₀`. This declaration itself does not export a real `HasDerivAt`; its ball-wide convergence is consumed by the downstream reduced-field derivative row. Friedli–Velenik Exercise 5.8 supplies the field weight, §5.4, Theorem 5.4, p. 224 supplies convergence, and §5.5, Theorem 5.8, p. 228 supplies parameter-analyticity context. The F6c local-limit assembly is a project extension. |
| §17.6.1 | Infinite-volume real reduced-field derivative from F6c | **Conditional / limited-range: implemented for nonempty general `A`, `∃ a₀ > 0`, `0 ≤ a < a₀`, and `0 < b < r < π/2`** | `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_general_hasDerivAt_field_high_temp` and `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_general_differentiableAt_field_high_temp` (`ClusterExpansion/FieldCorrelationInfiniteFieldDeriv.lean`) identify one F6c witness with `correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) Λ ⟨a,b',1⟩ A` throughout `b' ∈ (0,r)` by uniqueness against `fieldCorrelationℂAlongExhaustion_tendsto_at_real`, then restrict the complex derivative with `comp_ofReal` and `Complex.reCLM`. The variable is the reduced field `b = βh` with normalized `β = 1`; this does **not** cover `b = 0`, arbitrary physical-parameter rescaling, the full nonperturbative GJ range, or a U3/series derivative identity, sign, or uniform bound. Glimm–Jaffe Theorem 17.6.1, p. 313 is the motivation; the exact reduced-field bridge is a project-specific refinement of the Friedli–Velenik-supported F6c endpoint. |



> **Disclaimer (§18.x labels).** The "§18.x" tags in the rows below are a **project-internal analogy label**, NOT a literal correspondence to Glimm–Jaffe Chapter 18. GJ Ch. 18 (pp. 321–343) is the continuum P(φ)₂ Euclidean-QFT Schwinger-function cluster expansion and contains **no Ising/spin content**. The Lean work tagged "§18.x" is a genuine, axiom-free formalization of the **classical lattice high-temperature Kotecký–Preiss polymer / Mayer cluster expansion** for the Ising model, whose actual literature source is **Friedli–Velenik §3.7.3 (high-temperature expansion) and §5.4 / §5.7 (abstract Kotecký–Preiss polymer models)**. The GJ §18.x page numbers cited in these rows refer to the real GJ Chapter 18 (pp. 321–343) only as an analogy anchor.

Pair to Step 288 lower bound.
