The `piFinset` is the image of `s ×ˢ piFinset` under the injection `p.insertNth` (`Fin.insertNthEquiv`); the proof mirrors #4100 (`ext`, forward `x = ω p` / `ω' = Fin.removeNth p ω` via `Fin.insertNth_self_removeNth`, backward via `Fin.forall_iff_succAbove` + `insertNth_apply_same`/`_succAbove`) then `Finset.sum_map` + `Finset.sum_product`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Incompatibility-neighbourhood moment bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_{Q∼P} |Q|^d (e|t|)^|Q| ≤ |P|·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/PolymerActivityKPMoment.lean`. The moment generalisation of the KP neighbourhood bound (#4088), for the leaf-peel induction where a leaf absorbing `d` children carries a polynomial-moment factor `|Q|^d`. `incompatibilityActivity_cardPow_expWeighted_le` (`P ∈ allPolymers G`, `Δ²e|t| < 1`): `∑_{Q ∼ P} |Q|^d (e|t|)^|Q| ≤ |P|·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}` — each incompatible polymer is rooted at one of the `|supp P| ≤ |P|` support vertices (witness-count + `Finset.sum_comm` + subset, mirroring #4088), and the per-vertex sum is the moment bound `rootedPolymerActivity_cardPow_le` (#4099). The `d=0` case is `incompatibilityActivity_expWeighted_le` (up to `|supp P| ≤ |P|`). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Rooted-parent active-set scaffolding (leaf-peel induction)** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (active-vertex / closed / leaf predicates + erase-leaf lemmas)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActive.lean`. The combinatorial scaffolding for the rooted-tree KP leaf-peel induction, which recurses by removing a leaf of an active vertex set rather than re-deriving a shrinking tree's parent code. `rootedParentActiveVertices A := insert 0 (A.image Fin.succ)` (root `0` + active non-root vertices), `succ_mem_rootedParentActiveVertices`, `RootedParentActiveClosed par A` (every active vertex's parent is active/root), `RootedParentLeaf par A j` (`j ∈ A` is nobody's parent in `A`). The two induction-step facts: `RootedParentActiveClosed.erase_leaf` (erasing a leaf preserves active-closedness — an active `i ≠ j` cannot have parent `Fin.succ j` since `j` is a leaf) and `RootedParentLeaf.parent_mem_erase` (the leaf's parent stays active after the erase). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Leaf existence for the rooted-parent active-set induction** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (rank-decreasing parent ⟹ leaf in every nonempty active set)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentLeafExistence.lean`. The leaf-peel induction needs that a nonempty active set has a leaf. `exists_rootedParentLeaf_of_rank` (abstract: if `rank (par i) < rank (Fin.succ i)` for all `i`, then the maximal-rank active vertex is a leaf — any vertex it parented would have strictly larger rank, `Finset.exists_max_image` + `omega`). `completeGraphTreeParentCode_rank_lt` (the parent code strictly decreases the tree distance from the root, via `treeParent_spec`). `completeGraphTreeParentCode_exists_active_leaf` (specialising the rank criterion to the tree distance: every nonempty active set has a leaf for the complete-graph spanning-tree parent code). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The moment-weighted constrained active sum** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (definition + basic API)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveSum.lean`. The central object of the rooted-tree KP leaf-peel induction: `rootedParentActiveSum G par A hclosed k t` is the sum over labellings `ω : {v // v ∈ rootedParentActiveVertices A} → allPolymers G` (the active-vertex subtype) of the moment-weighted activity `∏_v |ω v|^{k v}·(e|t|)^{|ω v|}`, restricted to the labellings satisfying `ω (succ j) ∼ ω (par j)` for every active `j ∈ A` (the coercions `rootedParentActiveChild hj`, `rootedParentActiveParent hclosed hj` lift `succ j` / `par j` into the subtype via `succ_mem_rootedParentActiveVertices` / `hclosed`). `rootedParentActiveVertices_empty` (`= {0}`), `rootedParentActiveChild_coe`, `rootedParentActiveParent_coe`. The subtype `piFinset` with the dependent-membership constraint and the moment weight elaborates with automatic decidability. The base case (`A = ∅`, see next row), the active-subtype coordinate split, the leaf-peel inequality, and the strong induction follow. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Base case of the active sum (empty active set)** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`rootedParentActiveSum G par ∅ hclosed k t = ∑_P |P|^{k 0}(e|t|)^|P|`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveSum.lean`. The base case of the leaf-peel recursion: on the empty active set there are no constraints and only the root vertex `0`, so the active sum reduces to the root-only moment-weighted activity. `instance : Unique (RootedParentActive ∅)` (the only active vertex is `⟨0,_⟩`, since `rootedParentActiveVertices ∅ = {0}`). `rootedParentActiveSum_empty`: reindexes the singleton-domain `piFinset` sum (`ω ↦ ω default` / `P ↦ fun _ => P`, `Finset.sum_bij'`), discharges the vacuous constraint (`if_pos`), and collapses the singleton product (`Fintype.prod_unique`). This connects (in the specialization `k 0 = 0`) to the final target `allPolymersActivity G (e|t|) = ∑_P (e|t|)^|P|`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Leaf-split of the active-vertex subtype** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (insert/erase structural lemmas + `Option` equivalence + constant-`piFinset` split)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveSplit.lean`. The combinatorial backbone of the leaf-peel inductive step: peeling the leaf coordinate `Fin.succ j` off the active-vertex subtype. `rootedParentActiveVertices_insert_erase` (for `j ∈ A`, `rootedParentActiveVertices A = insert (Fin.succ j) (rootedParentActiveVertices (A.erase j))`, via `Finset.image_insert` + `Finset.insert_erase` + `Finset.insert_comm`), `succ_notMem_rootedParentActiveVertices_erase` (`Fin.succ j ∉ rootedParentActiveVertices (A.erase j)`, from `Finset.notMem_erase`). Combined through `Finset.subtypeInsertEquivOption` and `Equiv.subtypeEquivRight`, these give `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv hj : RootedParentActive A ≃ Option (RootedParentActive (A.erase j))` (leaf vertex `↦ none`, every other active vertex `↦ some`; `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv_symm_none_coe`/`_some_coe` record the coercions). The accompanying analytic tool `sum_piFinset_const_optionEquiv` (`e : α ≃ Option β`): a constant-`piFinset` sum `∑_{ω : α → X} f ω` over a fixed finset `s` splits as `∑_{x ∈ s} ∑_{η : β → X} f (fun a => (e a).elim x η)` (`Finset.sum_product'` + `Finset.sum_bij'` with the reconstruction `(e a).elim x η` and `Option.elim_none_some`). This is the coordinate split that will peel the leaf factor out of `rootedParentActiveSum`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Leaf-peel factorings of the active product/constraint** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (product split + constraint split)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeel.lean`. The two structural factorings the leaf-peel inductive step rests on. `prod_rootedParentActive_eq_mul` (for `j ∈ A`, any `CommMonoid`): `∏_{v : RootedParentActive A} g v = g (rootedParentActiveChild hj) · ∏_{w : RootedParentActive (A.erase j)} g ((rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv hj).symm (some w))` — the leaf factor at the peeled vertex `Fin.succ j` times the product over the erased active subtype, transported along the leaf-split equivalence (`Equiv.prod_comp` + `Fintype.prod_option`). `forall_mem_constraint_iff_erase`: the incompatibility constraints `ω (succ i) ∼ ω (par i)` ranging over all active `i ∈ A` factor as the single constraint at `j` together with those ranging over `A.erase j` (the `A = insert j (A.erase j)` forall-split; the `rootedParentActiveChild`/`rootedParentActiveParent` membership proofs are irrelevant, so the `i ∈ A.erase j` constraints lifted via `Finset.mem_of_mem_erase` coincide with those at `i ∈ A`). Together with `sum_piFinset_const_optionEquiv` these peel the leaf coordinate (value + own constraint + weight factor) out of `rootedParentActiveSum`, leaving the erased-subtype sum; the leaf sum is then bounded by the moment estimate #4102. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Forward action of the leaf-split equivalence** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (forward action + reconstruction evaluation)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveSplitApply.lean`. The leaf-peel assembly reconstructs a labelling `ω` of the active vertices of `A` as `ω v = (rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv hj v).elim x η` (from `sum_piFinset_const_optionEquiv`). To read off `ω` at each vertex it needs the *forward* action of the split equivalence (the `symm`-coercions only record the inverse). `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv_child`: the peeled vertex maps to `none` (`rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv hj (rootedParentActiveChild hj) = none`, via `Equiv.apply_symm_apply` on the `_symm_none_coe` identity). `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv_apply_some`: every active vertex `v` with `↑v ∈ rootedParentActiveVertices (A.erase j)` maps to `some ⟨↑v, _⟩` (`Equiv.apply_eq_iff_eq_symm_apply` + `Subtype.ext`). The reconstruction-evaluation corollaries `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv_recon_child` (`= x` at the leaf) and `rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv_recon_some` (`= η ⟨↑v, _⟩` elsewhere) read off the leaf value and the remainder values. These supply the per-vertex identifications the full coordinate-split of `rootedParentActiveSum` will use. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The leaf column sum and its KP bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (definition + identity + #4102 bound)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafColumn.lean`. Once the leaf coordinate is peeled out of `rootedParentActiveSum`, the leaf value `x` ranges over `allPolymers G` under the single constraint `x ∼ P`, with `P` the remainder polymer at the leaf's parent. `leafColumnSum G P d t := ∑_{x ∈ allPolymers G} (if x ∼ P then |x|^d·(e|t|)^{|x|} else 0)`. `leafColumnSum_eq`: this constrained sum equals the unconstrained moment sum over the incompatibility neighbourhood `incompatiblePolymers G P` (`Finset.sum_filter` + `Finset.filter_congr`, using `PolymersIncompatible.symm`). `leafColumnSum_le` (`P ∈ allPolymers G`, `Δ²·e·|t| < 1`): `leafColumnSum G P d t ≤ |P|·d!/(1 − Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`, directly from `incompatibilityActivity_cardPow_expWeighted_le` (#4102). The factor `|P|` is exactly the moment bump the leaf-peel induction folds into the remainder weight at the parent vertex. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Per-labelling leaf isolation** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (the coordinate-split assembly crux)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafInner.lean`. The assembly that peels the leaf coordinate out of `rootedParentActiveSum`. After `sum_piFinset_const_optionEquiv` reconstructs a labelling as `ω = fun a => (rootedParentActiveSplitEquiv hleaf.1 a).elim x η` (leaf value `x ∈ allPolymers G`, remainder labelling `η` of `RootedParentActive (A.erase j)`), `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_inner` shows the reconstructed summand summed over `x` factors as `(remainder summand) · leafColumnSum G (η ⟨par j, _⟩) (k (succ j)) t`, where the remainder summand is exactly the `rootedParentActiveSum` summand for `A.erase j` at `η`. Proof: per-`x`, the product factors via `prod_rootedParentActive_eq_mul` + reconstruction evaluation (#4109) into the leaf weight times the remainder product, and the constraint factors via `forall_mem_constraint_iff_erase` (#4108) + reconstruction evaluation into the leaf constraint `x ∼ η ⟨par j, _⟩` and the remainder constraints (membership-proof matching by `Subtype.ext rfl`); then the `if`/sum algebra (`Finset.mul_sum`, `if`-factoring) collects the leaf factor into `leafColumnSum` (#4110). This is the per-`η` step of the full coordinate split; summing over `η` then gives the leaf-peel decomposition of `rootedParentActiveSum`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The leaf-peel decomposition of the active sum** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (full coordinate split)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelStep.lean`. Summing the per-labelling leaf isolation (#4111) over the remainder labellings `η` gives the full leaf-peel decomposition: for a leaf `j`, `rootedParentActiveSum G par A hclosed k t = ∑_η (remainder summand at η) · leafColumnSum G (η ⟨par j, _⟩) (k (succ j)) t`, where the remainder summand is the `rootedParentActiveSum` summand for `A.erase j` at `η`. `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_peel`: the proof unfolds `rootedParentActiveSum`, applies `sum_piFinset_const_optionEquiv` (#4107) to split the leaf coordinate off the active-vertex labelling, commutes the two sums (`Finset.sum_comm`), and rewrites each inner sum by `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_inner` (#4111). The next step bounds each `leafColumnSum` factor by `leafColumnSum_le` (#4110), folding the `|η ⟨par j, _⟩|` moment bump into the remainder weight to obtain the leaf-peel inequality (against `rootedParentActiveSum` for `A.erase j` with `k` bumped at `par j`), and then the strong induction on `A.card`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The leaf-peel inequality for the active sum** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (one induction step)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelBound.lean`. Bounding each `leafColumnSum` factor of the leaf-peel decomposition (#4112) by `leafColumnSum_le` (#4110) and folding the resulting `\|η ⟨par j, _⟩\|` moment bump into the remainder weight gives the **leaf-peel inequality**: for a leaf `j` and `Δ²·e·\|t\| < 1`, `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_peel_le`: `rootedParentActiveSum G par A hclosed k t ≤ (k (succ j))!/(1 − Δ²e\|t\|)^{k (succ j)+1} · rootedParentActiveSum G par (A.erase j) _ (Function.update k (par j) (k (par j)+1)) t`. The moment bump is realised by `Function.update k (par j) (k (par j)+1)`: multiplying the remainder weight by `\|η ⟨par j, _⟩\|` raises the exponent at the unique active vertex `⟨par j, _⟩` of `A.erase j` by one (`Finset.prod_mul_distrib` + `Fintype.prod_ite_eq'` + `Function.update_self`/`update_of_ne` + `pow_succ`; the remainder summand is nonnegative, so `Finset.sum_le_sum` applies). This is one step of the leaf-peel recursion; iterating it (strong induction on `A.card`, base case `rootedParentActiveSum_empty`) yields the full moment-weighted bound. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The leaf-peel induction (child-count peel bound)** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (full recursion to a closed-form bound)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelInduction.lean`. Iterating the leaf-peel inequality (#4113) down to the empty active set yields a closed-form bound on `rootedParentActiveSum`. The invariant (avoiding bump bookkeeping): the exponent a vertex `v` carries when finally peeled is statically `k v + rootedParentChildCount par A v`, where `rootedParentChildCount par A v := #{i ∈ A | par i = v}` is the active out-degree. `rootedParentPeelFactor G t d := d!/(1 − Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`; `rootedParentActivePeelBound G par A k t := (∏_{j ∈ A} rootedParentPeelFactor G t (k (succ j) + childCount (succ j))) · ∑_P (P.card)^(k 0 + childCount 0)·(e|t|)^{P.card}`. `rootedParentActiveSum_le_childCount_bound` (assuming every nonempty active set has a leaf, e.g. a rank-decreasing parent, and `Δ²·e·|t| < 1`): `rootedParentActiveSum ≤ rootedParentActivePeelBound`, by strong induction on `A.card` (`Nat.strong_induction_on`). Helpers: `rootedParentChildCount_leaf_succ` (`= 0` at a leaf's `succ`), `rootedParentChildCount_erase` (erasing `j` drops `par j`'s count by one), and the recursion identity `rootedParentActivePeelBound_erase_update` (the `Function.update` bump at `par j` exactly compensates the child-count drop, via `hexp : k' v + childCount(A.erase j) v = k v + childCount A v` for all `v`, `Finset.mul_prod_erase`). This completes the rooted-tree leaf-peel machinery; the next step connects it to `mayerExpansionTerm` (#4095) and the per-site bound (#4087). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Leaf-peel bound for the complete-graph spanning-tree parent code** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (specialisation to univ + tree parent code)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelTree.lean`. Specialising the leaf-peel induction bound (#4114) to the full active set `Finset.univ` and the complete-graph spanning-tree parent code (`Penrose.completeGraphTreeParentCode`) discharges the leaf-existence hypothesis (via `completeGraphTreeParentCode_exists_active_leaf` — the parent code strictly decreases the tree distance from the root). `rootedParentActiveVertices_univ` (`= Finset.univ`, since `0` and the `Fin.succ i` exhaust `Fin (n+1)`), `rootedParentActiveClosed_univ` (any parent function is active-closed on `univ`). `rootedParentActiveSum_completeGraphTreeParentCode_univ_zero_le_peelBound` (`Δ²·e·|t| < 1`): the rooted-tree active sum at exponent `0` for a spanning tree `T` of the complete graph on `Fin (n+1)` is `≤` its child-count peel bound. This is the exact shape the Penrose tree-graph bound on `|mayerExpansionTerm|` (`mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_treeSum_rootedExpActivity`, #4095) feeds into: summing this over `T` and reindexing the polymer-sequence labellings bridges the Mayer term to the closed-form peel bound. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Reindexing full active-vertex labellings to `Fin (n+1)`** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (bridge infrastructure)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveUnivReindex.lean`. To feed the rooted-tree active sum into the Penrose tree-graph bound (whose labellings are `ω : Fin (n+1) → allPolymers G`), the full active-vertex subtype is reindexed to `Fin (n+1)`. `sum_piFinset_const_domEquiv` (`e : α ≃ β`): a constant-`piFinset` sum is invariant under reindexing the coordinate type, `∑_ω f ω = ∑_{ω'} f (ω' ∘ e)` (`Finset.sum_bij'` with precomposition by `e`/`e.symm`). `rootedParentActiveUnivEquiv : RootedParentActive Finset.univ ≃ Fin (n+1)` (the underlying coercion, via `rootedParentActiveVertices_univ` and `Equiv.subtypeUnivEquiv`), with coercion simp lemmas `rootedParentActiveUnivEquiv_apply`/`_symm_apply_coe`. These let the univ rooted-tree active sum (#4115) be rewritten as a sum over `Fin (n+1)`-labellings, matching the polymer-sequence labellings of the Penrose bound. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Univ rooted-tree active sum in `Fin (n+1)`-labelling form** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (explicit polymer-sequence form)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveUnivForm.lean`. `rootedParentActiveSum_univ_zero_eq`: for the full active set and any parent function, the rooted-tree active sum at exponent `0` equals `∑_{ω ∈ piFinset(Fin (n+1) → allPolymers G)} (if ∀ i, ω (Fin.succ i) ∼ ω (par i) then ∏_v (e|t|)^{|ω v|} else 0)` — the explicit polymer-sequence form with the parent-edge incompatibility constraint and the (unweighted, exponent `0`) activity product. Proof: reindex the active-vertex labellings to `Fin (n+1)` via `sum_piFinset_const_domEquiv` + `rootedParentActiveUnivEquiv` (#4116), then per labelling rewrite the constraint (`child`/`parent` coercions `Fin.succ i`/`par i`, forall over `univ`) and the product (`Equiv.prod_comp`, `pow_zero`). This shares the parent-constraint/product skeleton of the Penrose tree-graph bound's summand (`mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_treeSum_rootedExpActivity`, #4095), with an extra root `e` weight (the Penrose root weight is `|t|^{|ω 0|}`, here `(e|t|)^{|ω 0|}`) and without the per-tree sum; the next step is the Fubini swap identifying the Penrose tree sum with `∑_T` of this (the root `e` weight only strengthens the upper bound). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Fubini swap of the Penrose tree sum over complete-tree shapes** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (swap + parent-constraint relaxation)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveTreeBridge.lean`. `penroseTreeSum_le_subtype_parentConstraint` (`W ≥ 0`): the Penrose double sum `∑_ω ∑_{T ∈ ST(incompat ω)} W ω` over polymer sequences and spanning trees of the incompatibility graph is `≤ ∑_{T : ST(⊤)} ∑_{ω : parent-constraint(T)} W ω`, the sum over complete-graph spanning-tree shapes `T` of the weight over sequences satisfying the per-edge parent incompatibility `ω (Fin.succ i) ∼ ω (completeGraphTreeParentCode n T i)`. Proof: each inner spanning-tree sum is rewritten as a subtype sum over complete-graph trees (`Finset.sum_coe_sort`, `Finset.filter_mem_eq_inter`, `Finset.inter_eq_right` with `spanningTreeEdgeSubsets_mono le_top` — spanning trees of `incompat ω` are spanning trees of `⊤`), the two sums swap (`Finset.sum_comm`), and each inner sum is relaxed to the parent-edge constraint via `sum_filter_treeIncompat_le_filter_parentConstraint` (#4096). This bridges the Penrose tree-graph bound on `|mayerExpansionTerm|` (#4095) to the per-tree parent-constraint sums that `rootedParentActiveSum_univ_zero_eq` (#4117) identifies with the rooted-tree active sum. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Penrose tree sum bounded by the summed child-count peel bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (bridge chain)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveTreePeelBound.lean`. `penroseTreeSum_le_sum_peelBound` (`Δ²·e·|t| < 1`): the Penrose tree-graph sum `∑_ω ∑_{T ∈ ST(incompat ω)} |t|^{|ω 0|} ∏_i e^{|ω(succ i)|}|t|^{|ω(succ i)|}` (the per-`(n+1)` weight of the Mayer-term bound #4095) is `≤ ∑_{T : ST(⊤)} rootedParentActivePeelBound G (completeGraphTreeParentCode n T) univ (fun _ => 0) t`. Chains: the Fubini swap (#4118) to per-tree parent-constraint sums; per tree, the Penrose root weight `|t|^{|ω 0|} ≤ (e|t|)^{|ω 0|}` (since `1 ≤ e`, `pow_le_pow_left₀` + `le_mul_of_one_le_left`) and `e^c|t|^c = (e|t|)^c` (`mul_pow`) turn the constrained activity sum into the `Fin (n+1)`-labelling form (`Finset.sum_filter`), identified with the rooted-tree active sum (`rootedParentActiveSum_univ_zero_eq`, #4117), which the leaf-peel induction bounds by the child-count peel bound (`rootedParentActiveSum_completeGraphTreeParentCode_univ_zero_le_peelBound`, #4115). The remaining step multiplies by `(n+1)!⁻¹` to bound `|mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t|` and sums the peel bound over spanning-tree shapes. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Mayer expansion term bounded by the summed child-count peel bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (Mayer-term ⟶ peel bound)** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTermPeelBound.lean`. `mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_sum_peelBound` (`Δ²·e·|t| < 1`): `|mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t| ≤ (n+1)!⁻¹ · ∑_{T : ST(⊤)} rootedParentActivePeelBound G (completeGraphTreeParentCode n T) univ (fun _ => 0) t`. Composes the Penrose tree-graph bound on the Mayer term (`mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_treeSum_rootedExpActivity`, #4095) with the leaf-peel bridge bounding the Penrose tree sum by the summed child-count peel bound (`penroseTreeSum_le_sum_peelBound`, #4119), via `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left` (the `(n+1)!⁻¹` prefactor is nonnegative). The Mayer expansion term is now bounded by the closed-form leaf-peel machinery; the remaining step is the labelled-tree child-count closing — evaluating/bounding `∑_{T : ST(⊤)} peelBound` against the `(n+1)!⁻¹` prefactor (the tree count `(n+1)^{n-1}` and child-count factorials) — and the ensuing summability/convergence. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Sharpened (tail) per-vertex polymer-activity moment bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_{Q∋v} |Q|^d u^{|Q|} ≤ (Δ²u)·d!/(1−Δ²u)^{d+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/PolymerActivityTailMoment.lean` (+ `GeometricMoment.lean` tail lemma). Since every polymer is nonempty (`|Q| ≥ 1`), the `ℓ = 0` term of the underlying geometric series is absent, so the per-vertex moment bound (`rootedPolymerActivity_cardPow_le`, #4099) sharpens to carry an extra factor `Δ²u`: `tsum_succ_pow_mul_geometric_succ_le` (`0 ≤ r < 1`): `∑_ℓ (ℓ+1)^d r^{ℓ+1} ≤ r·d!/(1−r)^{d+1}` (each `(ℓ+1)^d ≤ d!·C(ℓ+d,d)` directly via `Nat.pow_sub_le_descFactorial`, factor `r` pulled out of `r^{ℓ+1}`); `rootedPolymerActivity_cardPow_tail_le` (`Δ²u < 1`): `∑_{Q ∋ v} |Q|^d u^{|Q|} ≤ (Δ²u)·d!/(1−Δ²u)^{d+1}` (fiber over polymer size, drop the empty `ℓ = 0` fiber via `IsPolymer.nonempty`, reindex `ℓ ↦ ℓ+1` with `Finset.sum_range_succ'`, apply the tail geometric bound). This `Δ²u` factor — one per non-root vertex of the rooted-tree leaf-peel — is an ingredient for a convergent (summable-over-`n`) cluster-expansion bound (which also requires the tree/child-count combinatorics and a smallness condition), since the bare `d!/(1−Δ²u)^{d+1}` factors do not decay with the tree size. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Sharpened (tail) incompatibility-neighbourhood moment bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_{Q∼P} |Q|^d (e|t|)^|Q| ≤ |P|·(Δ²e|t|)·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/PolymerActivityKPMomentTail.lean`. The tail sharpening of the incompatibility-neighbourhood moment bound (`incompatibilityActivity_cardPow_expWeighted_le`, #4102): replacing the per-vertex moment bound by its tail version (`rootedPolymerActivity_cardPow_tail_le`, #4121) — valid since every incompatible polymer is nonempty — carries an extra factor `Δ²e|t|`. `incompatibilityActivity_cardPow_expWeighted_tail_le` (`P ∈ allPolymers G`, `Δ²e|t| < 1`): `∑_{Q ∼ P} |Q|^d (e|t|)^{|Q|} ≤ |P|·(Δ²e|t|)·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`. The reduction to per-support-vertex moment sums (each incompatible polymer rooted at a shared support vertex, `|supp P| ≤ |P|`) is identical to #4102; only the per-vertex bound is sharpened. This supplies the per-vertex `Δ²e|t|` smallness factor for the convergent leaf-peel closing. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Sharpened (tail) leaf column sum bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`leafColumnSum G P d t ≤ |P|·(Δ²e|t|)·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafColumnTail.lean`. The tail sharpening of `leafColumnSum_le` (#4110): using the tail incompatibility-neighbourhood moment bound (`incompatibilityActivity_cardPow_expWeighted_tail_le`, #4122) instead of #4102 carries an extra factor `Δ²e|t|`. `leafColumnSum_tail_le` (`P ∈ allPolymers G`, `Δ²e|t| < 1`): `leafColumnSum G P d t ≤ |P|·(Δ²e|t|)·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}` (`leafColumnSum_eq` + #4122). This propagates the per-vertex `Δ²e|t|` smallness factor into the leaf-peel column sums, toward a sharpened (summable-over-`n`) peel bound. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Relaxing the tree child-count factorial sum to all parent functions** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (Prüfer-free tree → parent-function relaxation)** | `ClusterExpansion/Penrose/ParentCodeFactorialSum.lean`. The convergent cluster-expansion closing needs a summable bound on `∑_{T : ST(K_{n+1})} ∏_v (childCount T v)!`. Because the complete-graph spanning-tree parent code injects spanning trees into parent functions `Fin n → Fin (n+1)` (`completeGraphTreeParentCode_injective`), and the child count `rootedParentChildCount (completeGraphTreeParentCode n T) univ` is exactly the parent-function fiber size, `sum_completeGraphTrees_prod_childCount_factorial_le`: `∑_{T} ∏_v (childCount T v)! ≤ ∑_{p : Fin n → Fin (n+1)} ∏_v (fiber count of p at v)!` (`Finset.sum_image` on the injection + `Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset`, monotone in ℕ). This Prüfer-free relaxation isolates the combinatorial evaluation of the parent-function sum (which equals `(2n)!/n! = n!·C(2n,n) ≤ 4^n·n!`, via the central-binomial bound `Nat.centralBinom_le_four_pow`) from the tree-specific machinery — the route to a `4r/(1−r)² < 1` summable cluster-expansion convergence. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Parent-function fiber-factorial sum = rising factorial** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_p ∏_v (#{i\|p i=v})! = ascFactorial m n`; `m=n+1` ⟹ `≤ 4^n·n!`)** | `ClusterExpansion/Penrose/FiberFactorialSum.lean`. The closed-form evaluation of the parent-function sum left open by #4124. `fiberCount n m p v := #{i \| p i = v}`; `parentFiberFactorialSum n m := ∑_{p:Fin n→Fin m} ∏_v (fiberCount p v)!`. `parentFiberFactorialSum_eq_ascFactorial`: `= Nat.ascFactorial m n = m·(m+1)···(m+n−1)`, by induction on `n` — a function `Fin (n+1)→Fin m` splits via `Fin.snocEquiv` into a last value `a` and initial tuple `p'`; the fiber at `v` gains `+1` iff `v=a` (`fiberCount_snoc`, via `Fin.sum_univ_castSucc`), so the product gains a factor `fiberCount p' a + 1` (`prod_fiberCount_factorial_snoc`, `Finset.mul_prod_erase` + `Nat.factorial_succ`), and summing over `a` multiplies by `∑_a (fiberCount p' a + 1) = n + m` (`sum_fiberCount` = `n` via `card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise`), matching `Nat.ascFactorial_succ`. `parentFiberFactorialSum_succ_le_four_pow_mul_factorial` (`m = n+1`): `= (2n)!/n! = n!·C(2n,n) ≤ 4^n·n!` (`Nat.factorial_mul_ascFactorial` for `(2n)!`, `Nat.choose_mul_factorial_mul_factorial` for the central-binomial split, `Nat.centralBinom_le_four_pow`). This is the Prüfer-free combinatorial heart of the convergent cluster-expansion closing: combined with the tree→parent-function relaxation (#4124) it gives `∑_{T:ST(⊤)} ∏_v (childCount T v)! ≤ 4^n·n!`, the `4^n` control the `r^n` tail factor needs. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Tree child-count factorial sum ≤ 4^n·n!** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_{T:ST(⊤)} ∏_v (childCount T v)! ≤ 4^n·n!`)** | `ClusterExpansion/Penrose/TreeChildCountFactorialBound.lean`. Composes the tree → parent-function relaxation (#4124) with the closed-form parent-function evaluation (#4125): `sum_completeGraphTrees_prod_childCount_factorial_le_four_pow_mul_factorial`: `∑_{T : ST(K_{n+1})} ∏_v (childCount T v)! ≤ 4^n·n!`. The bridge is that `rootedParentChildCount p univ` and `fiberCount n (n+1) p` are the same function (both `#{i | p i = v}`), so the relaxed parent-function sum is definitionally `parentFiberFactorialSum n (n+1)`, which #4125 evaluates as `(2n)!/n! ≤ 4^n·n!`. This is the completed combinatorial control on the spanning-tree factorial weights — the `4^n` factor that, combined with the per-vertex `r` tail factor and the `(n+1)!⁻¹` Mayer prefactor, gives the `4r/(1−r)² < 1` summable cluster-expansion convergence. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The root moment bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`∑_{P∈allPolymers} |P|^d (e|t|)^|P| ≤ |V|·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootMomentBound.lean`. In the leaf-peel child-count peel bound the root vertex carries the moment sum `∑_{P∈allPolymers G} |P|^d (e|t|)^{|P|}` (the base case `rootedParentActiveSum_empty`). `sum_allPolymers_cardPow_expWeighted_le` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`): `∑_{P∈allPolymers G} |P|^d (e|t|)^{|P|} ≤ |V|·d!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{d+1}` (`|V| = Fintype.card ι`). Proof: every polymer is rooted at each of its `|supp P| ≥ 1` support vertices, so `∑_P f(P) ≤ ∑_P |supp P|·f(P) = ∑_v ∑_{P∋v} f(P)` (`one_le_card_polymerSupport_of_mem_allPolymers` + `Finset.sum_filter`/`filter_univ_mem` + `Finset.sum_comm`), bounded per vertex by the per-vertex moment bound `rootedPolymerActivity_cardPow_le` (#4099) summed over the `|V|` vertices. This bounds the root factor of the peel bound, the last analytic ingredient before the convergent capstone (combined with the `4^n·n!` tree control #4126 and the per-vertex `r` tail factor). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The sharpened (tail) leaf-peel inequality** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (one induction step with extra `Δ²e|t|`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelTailBound.lean`. The tail sharpening of the leaf-peel inequality (#4113): bounding each `leafColumnSum` factor by its tail estimate (`leafColumnSum_tail_le`, #4123, which carries an extra factor `Δ²e|t|`) gives one induction step with an extra `Δ²e|t|`: `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_peel_tail_le` (leaf `j`, `Δ²e|t| < 1`): `rootedParentActiveSum G par A hclosed k t ≤ (Δ²e|t|)·(k (succ j))!/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{k (succ j)+1} · rootedParentActiveSum G par (A.erase j) _ (update k (par j) (k (par j)+1)) t`. Identical to #4113 (decomposition via `rootedParentActiveSum_leaf_peel` #4112, moment bump via `Function.update`) except using `leafColumnSum_tail_le` instead of `leafColumnSum_le`. The extra `Δ²e|t|` per peeled (non-root) vertex is needed for the later summability argument over the Mayer order `n` (with the `4^n·n!` tree control, root moment, and high-temperature condition). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The sharpened (tail) leaf-peel induction** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`rootedParentActiveSum ≤ (Δ²e|t|)^{|A|}·peelBound`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelTailInduction.lean`. Iterating the tail leaf-peel inequality (#4128) down to the empty active set, reusing the peel-bound recursion identity (`rootedParentActivePeelBound_erase_update`, #4114), gives the child-count peel bound with an extra factor `(Δ²e|t|)^{|A|}` — one `Δ²e|t|` per peeled (non-root) vertex. `rootedParentActiveSum_le_pow_mul_childCount_bound` (every nonempty active set has a leaf, `Δ²e|t| < 1`): `rootedParentActiveSum G par A hclosed k t ≤ (Δ²e|t|)^{|A|}·rootedParentActivePeelBound G par A k t`, by strong induction on `A.card`. Base `A = ∅`: `(Δ²e|t|)^0·peelBound(∅) = rootedParentActiveSum_empty`. Step: `rootedParentActiveSum(A) ≤ (Δ²e|t|)·peelFactor·rootedParentActiveSum(A.erase j)` (#4128 tail inequality) `≤ (Δ²e|t|)·peelFactor·(Δ²e|t|)^{|A|−1}·peelBound(A.erase j)` (IH) `= (Δ²e|t|)^{|A|}·peelBound(A)` (`pow_succ` on `|A| = |A.erase j|+1` + #4114's `rootedParentActivePeelBound_erase_update`). The `(Δ²e|t|)^{|A|}` factor is the source of the geometric `(4r)^n` decay in the cluster-expansion convergence. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The sharpened (tail) tree leaf-peel bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`rootedParentActiveSum(parentCode T, univ, 0) ≤ (Δ²e|t|)^n·peelBound`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveLeafPeelTailTree.lean`. The tail analog of #4115: specialising the tail leaf-peel induction (`rootedParentActiveSum_le_pow_mul_childCount_bound`, #4129) to the full active set (cardinality `n`, via `Finset.card_univ` + `Fintype.card_fin`) and the complete-graph spanning-tree parent code, discharging leaf existence via `completeGraphTreeParentCode_exists_active_leaf`. `rootedParentActiveSum_completeGraphTreeParentCode_univ_zero_le_pow_mul_peelBound` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`): `rootedParentActiveSum G (parentCode T) univ _ (fun _ => 0) t ≤ (Δ²e|t|)^n · peelBound`. The `(Δ²e|t|)^n` factor — one per non-root vertex of the spanning tree — feeds the geometric `(4r)^n` decay. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The peel bound in factorial-product form** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (`peelBound ≤ (|V|·∏_v (childCount v)!)/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{2n+1}`)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActivePeelBoundFactorial.lean`. Bounding the root moment factor of the child-count peel bound by the root moment bound (#4127) and collecting the per-vertex `(1−r)` powers (`r = Δ²e|t|`) recasts the peel bound (full active set, exponent `0`) as a factorial product. `rootedParentActivePeelBound_univ_zero_le_card_mul_prod_childCount_factorial_div` (`0 < 1−Δ²e|t|`): `rootedParentActivePeelBound G par univ (fun _ => 0) t ≤ (|V|·∏_v (childCount v)!)/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{2n+1}`. Proof: the product of peel factors `∏_j (d_{succ j}!/(1−r)^{d_{succ j}+1})` collects (via `Finset.prod_div_distrib` + `Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum`) into `(∏_j d_{succ j}!)/(1−r)^{∑_j (d_{succ j}+1)}`; the root moment factor is bounded by #4127 (`≤ |V|·d_0!/(1−r)^{d_0+1}`); the per-vertex exponents sum to `2n+1` since the child counts sum to `n` (one parent-edge per non-root vertex) (`∑_v childCount v = n` via `Finset.card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise`, `Fin.sum_univ_succ`), and `∏_v d_v! = d_0!·∏_j d_{succ j}!` (`Fin.prod_univ_succ`). This is the form that pairs with the spanning-tree factorial bound `∑_T ∏_v (childCount v)! ≤ 4^n·n!` (#4126). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The Penrose tree sum bounded by the (Δ²e|t|)^n-weighted peel bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (tail of #4119)** | `ClusterExpansion/RootedParentActiveTreePeelBoundTail.lean`. The tail sharpening of `penroseTreeSum_le_sum_peelBound` (#4119): using the sharpened (tail) tree leaf-peel bound (#4130) at the final step. `penroseTreeSum_le_sum_pow_peelBound` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`): the Penrose tree-graph sum (the per-`(n+1)` weight of the Mayer-term bound #4095) is `≤ ∑_{T:ST(⊤)} (Δ²e|t|)^n · rootedParentActivePeelBound G (parentCode T) univ (fun _ => 0) t`. Identical to #4119 (Fubini swap #4118, root `e`-weight domination, `rootedParentActiveSum_univ_zero_eq` #4117) except the final per-`T` bound uses `rootedParentActiveSum_completeGraphTreeParentCode_univ_zero_le_pow_mul_peelBound` (#4130) instead of #4115, contributing the `(Δ²e|t|)^n` factor per shape. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **The Mayer term bounded by the (Δ²e|t|)^n-weighted summed peel bound** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (tail of #4120)** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTermPeelBoundTail.lean`. The tail sharpening of `mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_sum_peelBound` (#4120): composing the Penrose tree-graph bound on the Mayer term (#4095) with the tail Penrose tree-sum bound (`penroseTreeSum_le_sum_pow_peelBound`, #4132). `mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_sum_pow_peelBound` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`): `|mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t| ≤ (n+1)!⁻¹ · ∑_{T:ST(⊤)} (Δ²e|t|)^n · rootedParentActivePeelBound G (parentCode T) univ (fun _ => 0) t` (`mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`, `(n+1)!⁻¹ ≥ 0`). The Mayer term is now bounded by the `(Δ²e|t|)^n`-weighted peel-bound sum; combining with the peel-bound factorial form (#4131) and the tree factorial bound (#4126) gives the summable geometric `(4r/(1−r)²)^n` bound. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **★ Summability of the Mayer expansion terms (sufficient-condition KP convergence)** (Issue #3954) | **Implemented (absolute summability of `n ↦ \|mayerExpansionTerm G n t\|` under the sufficient condition `4r/(1−r)² < 1`)** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTermTailSummability.lean`. The convergence capstone, assembling the entire §18.5 KP machinery. `sum_pow_rootedParentActivePeelBound_le`: `∑_T (Δ²e|t|)^n·peelBound ≤ ((Δ²e|t|)^n·|V|·4^n·n!)/(1−Δ²e|t|)^{2n+1}` (the factorial-product peel bound #4131 summed over trees, with the tree factorial bound #4126 cast ℕ→ℝ via `Nat.cast_sum`/`Nat.cast_prod`, and `(1−r)`/`|V|` factored out by `Finset.mul_sum` + `mul_div_right_comm`). `mayerExpansionTerm_succ_abs_le_card_div_mul_geometric` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`): the geometric per-order bound `|mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t| ≤ |V|/(1−r)·(4r/(1−r)²)^n` (composing the tail Mayer bound #4133 with the summed peel bound, using `(n+1)!⁻¹·n! = n!/(n+1)! ≤ 1` and the power identity `(1−r)^{2n+1} = ((1−r)²)^n·(1−r)`, closed by `field_simp`/`ring`). `summable_abs_mayerExpansionTerm_succ_of_tail_condition` (`Δ²e|t| < 1` ∧ `4·Δ²e|t|/(1−Δ²e|t|)² < 1`): `Summable (fun n => |mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t|)`, via the geometric majorant `|V|/(1−r)·(4r/(1−r)²)^n` (`summable_geometric_of_lt_one` + `Summable.of_nonneg_of_le`). `summable_abs_mayerExpansionTerm_of_tail_condition`: the full series `n ↦ |mayerExpansionTerm G n t|` (including head terms) is summable (`summable_nat_add_iff`). This is a **sufficient-condition** high-temperature tail-summability result for the Mayer expansion terms on a fixed finite graph (a Kotecký–Preiss-type bound in the spirit of FV §5.4); it is not the sharp KP criterion, nor the convergence of the pressure/free-energy series, nor the thermodynamic limit. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Convergence of the Mayer expansion series** (Issue #3954) | **Conditional / limited-range: Mayer partial sums converge in the explicit regime** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerSeriesConvergence.lean`. From the absolute summability of the Mayer expansion terms (#4134), under `4Δ²e|t|/(1−Δ²e|t|)² < 1`: `summable_mayerExpansionTerm_of_tail_condition`: `Summable (fun n => mayerExpansionTerm G n t)` (not merely absolutely; `Summable.of_abs`). `tendsto_mayerPartialSum_of_tail_condition`: `Tendsto (fun N => mayerPartialSum G N t) atTop (𝓝 (∑'_n mayerExpansionTerm G n t))` — the Mayer partial sums `∑_{n=0}^N mayerExpansionTerm G n t` converge to the Mayer series (`HasSum.tendsto_sum_nat` composed with `tendsto_add_atTop_nat 1` for the `range (N+1)` indexing). This discharges the "convergence follows from Kotecký–Preiss-type bounds (deferred)" note on `mayerPartialSum` in the explicit high-temperature regime. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Explicit bound on the Mayer expansion sum** (Issue #3954) | **Conditional / limited-range: `∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t| ≤ |V|/((1−r)(1−ρ))`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTsumBound.lean`. Summing the geometric per-order bound (#4134) over `n`: `tsum_abs_mayerExpansionTerm_succ_le` (`Δ²e|t| < 1` ∧ `ρ := 4r/(1−r)² < 1`, `r = Δ²e|t|`): `∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t| ≤ |V|/(1−r)·(1−ρ)⁻¹` (`Summable.tsum_le_tsum` with the per-order bound + `tsum_mul_left` + `tsum_geometric_of_lt_one`). This is the explicit Kotecký–Preiss-type bound on the convergent cluster-expansion sum (the total contribution of the non-trivial Mayer orders), quantifying the term-summability of #4134. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | **Volume-uniform (per-site) Mayer expansion sum bound** (Issue #3954) | **Conditional / limited-range: `(∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t|)/|V| ≤ ((1−r)(1−ρ))⁻¹`, volume-uniform RHS** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTsumPerSite.lean`. Dividing the explicit Mayer expansion sum bound (#4136) by the volume `|V| = Fintype.card ι`: `tsum_abs_mayerExpansionTerm_succ_div_card_le` (nonempty `ι`, `Δ²e|t| < 1`, `ρ := 4r/(1−r)² < 1`, `r = Δ²e|t|`): `(∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm G (n+1) t|)/|V| ≤ ((1−r)(1−ρ))⁻¹` (`div_le_iff₀` + the #4136 bound). The right-hand side is **independent of the volume**, and the Kotecký–Preiss condition `4Δ²e|t|/(1−Δ²e|t|)² < 1` itself depends only on the maximum degree `Δ` (volume-uniform on bounded-degree graphs) — so this is the volume-uniform convergence of the per-site cluster-expansion contribution, addressing the "volume-uniform convergence open" gap of the §18.5 convergence row via the KP tree-graph route. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.6 analogy | **Complex Mayer expansion term (holomorphic)** (Issue #4149) | **Implemented (complexification + per-term entire/analytic)** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/TermsComplex.lean`. The complex-analytic foundation for the per-site-KP Mayer–Montroll identity via analytic continuation (Issue #4149): complexifies the (real-polynomial-in-`t`) Mayer expansion term so it can be shown holomorphic. `clusterSeqActivityComplex z ω := ∏_{i:Fin n} z^{|ω i|}` and `mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z := ∑_ω (ursellCoefficient ω : ℂ)·clusterSeqActivityComplex z ω` (the `ursellCoefficient` is `t`-independent). `_ofReal` bridges `clusterSeqActivityComplex (↑t) ω = ↑(clusterSeqActivity t ω)` and `mayerExpansionTermComplex G n (↑t) = ↑(mayerExpansionTerm G n t)` (via `Complex.ofReal_{prod,pow,sum,mul}`). Each term is **entire**: `clusterSeqActivityComplex_differentiable` / `mayerExpansionTermComplex_differentiable` (`Differentiable ℂ`, finite product/sum of `z ↦ z^k`), hence `mayerExpansionTermComplex_analyticOnNhd` (`AnalyticOnNhd ℂ … Set.univ`, via `DifferentiableOn.analyticOnNhd`) and `mayerExpansionTermComplex_differentiableOn`. This is PR-A of the §18.6 analytic-continuation plan (next: the complex Mayer tsum holomorphic on a ball via `Complex.differentiableOn_tsum_of_summable_norm`, then the identity theorem on the per-site KP interval). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Complex Mayer tsum holomorphic on a ball (Weierstrass)** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-B: `DifferentiableOn ℂ (∑'_n mayerExpansionTermComplex G n ·)` on `ball 0 R` under per-site KP at `R`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/TermsComplexHolomorphic.lean`. PR-B of the §18.6 plan: the complex Mayer series is holomorphic on the per-site Kotecký–Preiss ball, by a uniform summable majorant (Weierstrass M-test). **Norm bound** `mayerExpansionTermComplex_norm_le_termAbsSum`: `‖mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z‖ ≤ ∑_ω |ursellCoefficient ω|·clusterSeqActivity ‖z‖ ω` (`norm_sum_le` + `clusterSeqActivityComplex_norm`: `‖∏_i z^{|ω i|}‖ = ∏_i ‖z‖^{|ω i|}`) — the same term-absolute-sum that bounds `|mayerExpansionTerm G n ‖z‖|`. The geometric bound on it (`termAbsSum_le_treeSum_activity` extracting the per-`ω` `|ursell|·activity ≤ tree` step, then the existing `penroseTreeSum_le_sum_pow_peelBound` / `sum_pow_rootedParentActivePeelBound_le` chain reused) gives `mayerExpansionTermComplex_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric`: `‖mayerExpansionTermComplex G (n+1) z‖ ≤ (|ι|/(1−r))·(4r/(1−r)²)^n`, `r = Δ²e‖z‖`. With the majorant's monotonicity in `r` (`geometricMajorant_mono_of_le`) and the `n=0` head term (constant in `z`) added via `summable_nat_add_iff`, the **headline** `mayerExpansionTermComplex_tsum_differentiableOn_ball` (`0 ≤ R`, per-site KP at `R`): `DifferentiableOn ℂ (fun z => ∑'_n mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z) (Metric.ball 0 R)` (`Complex.differentiableOn_tsum_of_summable_norm`). Next (PR-C): `ofReal_tsum` + the identity theorem on `Ico 0 T'` give the volume-uniform real identity `polymerFreeEnergy = ∑'_n mayerExpansionTerm`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340. |
| §18.5 / §18.6 analogy | **Volume-uniform per-site Kotecký–Preiss Mayer–Montroll identity** (Issue #4149, capstone) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-C: `polymerFreeEnergy G t = ∑'_n mayerExpansionTerm G n t` on `Ico 0 T` for any per-site-KP `T`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/MayerIdentityPersiteKP.lean`. **The genuine upgrade of the Mayer–Montroll identity to the volume-uniform regime.** The existing `mayer_identity_general_t` holds only under the **volume-dependent** hypotheses `e·(total activity) < 1` / `|Ξ−1| < 1` (the naive `log(1+ε)` Taylor radius), which fail at large volume. Here the identity is extended to **any** per-site Kotecký–Preiss interval `Ico 0 T` — any `T > 0` satisfying the **volume-uniform** conditions `Δ²·e·T < 1` and `4r/(1−r)² < 1` (`r = Δ²eT`, equivalently `Δ²eT < 3−2√2`), which depend only on the maximum degree `Δ` — by **analytic continuation**: both sides are real-analytic on `Ico 0 T` and agree on a neighbourhood of `0` (`mayer_identity_general_t_eventually`), so they agree throughout by the identity theorem (`AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_eventuallyEq`, `isPreconnected_Ico`). LHS analyticity is `polymerFreeEnergy_analyticOnNhd_Ici_zero` (`log Ξ`, `Ξ ≥ 1 > 0`); **RHS analyticity** `polymerFreeEnergy_analyticOnNhd_mayer_tsum_Ioo` / `_Ico`: the real Mayer tsum is `t ↦ (F ↑t).re` for the holomorphic complex tsum `F` (#4151), real-analytic by `AnalyticOnNhd.re_ofReal` (with `Complex.ofReal_tsum` + `mayerExpansionTermComplex_ofReal` identifying `↑(∑'_n mayerExpansionTerm) = F ↑t`). **Headline** `polymerFreeEnergy_eq_tsum_mayerExpansionTerm_of_persite_kp` (`0 < T`, per-site KP at `T`, `t ∈ Ico 0 T`). This closes the volume-dependence gap (Issue #4149) — the volume-uniform Mayer-series representation of the polymer free energy, the input for the §18.6 infinite-volume free-energy analyticity. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Volume-uniform per-site lattice free-energy bound** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D1: `\|freeEnergy − (log2 + edge-density·log cosh)\| ≤ kpBound (2d)(tanh βJ)`, `Λ`-independent** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/LatticeFreeEnergyKPBound.lean`. Composes the per-site-KP Mayer identity (#4152) with the volume-uniform Mayer bound (#4148) into a **box-independent** bound on the cluster-expansion part of the lattice free energy. `latticeGraph_freeEnergy_deviation_le_kpBound` (`0 ≤ J`, `0 < β`, per-site KP conditions at `2d`/`T`, `tanh βJ < T`): `|freeEnergy (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ) ⟨J,0,β⟩ − (log 2 + (|E|/|Λ|)·log cosh(βJ))| ≤ kpBound (2d) (tanh βJ)`. Proof: `freeEnergy_eq_polymerFreeEnergy` isolates the deviation as `polymerFreeEnergy G (tanh βJ)/|Λ|`; the per-site-KP identity (#4152, with the actual `maxDegree ≤ 2d` KP hypotheses discharged from the `2d` ones by `kpRegion_downward_closed`) rewrites `polymerFreeEnergy = ∑'_n mayerExpansionTerm`; `mayerExpansionTerm_zero` shifts off the `n=0` term, `norm_tsum_le_tsum_norm` gives `|∑'| ≤ ∑'|·|`, and #4148 bounds `(∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm (n+1)|)/|Λ| ≤ kpBound (2d) (tanh βJ)`. The RHS depends only on `d` and the temperature — the volume-uniform input for the §18.6 infinite-volume coupling analyticity. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Infinite-volume real-axis KP free-energy bound** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.1: `\|freeEnergyInfinite − (log2 + d·log cosh βJ)\| ≤ kpBound (2d)(tanh βJ)`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/LatticeFreeEnergyInfiniteKPBound.lean`. Passes the volume-uniform per-site bound (#4153) to the cubic thermodynamic limit. **Cubic edge-density limit** `tendsto_inducedLatticeGraph_cubicBox_edgeDensity`: `(inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) (cubicBox d n)).edgeFinset.card / (cubicBox d n).card → d`, by a squeeze — upper `≤ d` (`inducedLatticeGraph_card_edgeFinset_le`, i.e. `2|E| = ∑ deg ≤ 2d|V|`), lower from interior vertices having full degree `2d` (`inducedLatticeGraph_cubicBox_interior_degree_eq`: an interior vertex's `2d` lattice neighbours `v ± e_j` all lie in the box, injected via `Function.update`) so `2|E| ≥ 2d·|cubicBox d n|` at stage `n+1` (`inducedLatticeGraph_cubicBox_card_edgeFinset_ge`), with the box ratio `(2n+1)^d/(2n+3)^d → 1`. **Headline** `freeEnergyInfinite_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_deviation_le_kpBound` (`0 ≤ J`, `0 < β`, per-site KP at `2d`/`T`, `tanh βJ < T`): the stagewise deviation (bounded by `kpBound` via #4153) converges (`freeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_tendsto` + the edge-density limit) to `freeEnergyInfinite − (log2 + d·log cosh βJ)`, so `le_of_tendsto` transfers the bound: `|freeEnergyInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩ − (log 2 + d·log cosh(βJ))| ≤ kpBound (2d)(tanh βJ)`. The real-axis target for the §18.6 infinite-volume coupling analyticity (PR-D2.2+). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Complex volume-uniform per-site Mayer bound** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.2: `(∑'_n ‖mayerExpansionTermComplex … (n+1) z‖)/\|Λ\| ≤ kpBound (2d) R` on `ball 0 R`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/TermsComplexPerSiteBound.lean`. The complex-norm analog of the real per-site Mayer bound (#4137/#4148) — the ball-uniform Montel input for the infinite-volume holomorphic limit (PR-D2.3). `mayerExpansionTermComplex_zero` (`mayerExpansionTermComplex G 0 z = 0`, since the empty `ω` has `ursellCoefficient = 0` on the disconnected `Fin 0` graph). Summing the complex geometric bound (`mayerExpansionTermComplex_succ_norm_le_card_div_mul_geometric`, #4151) over `n`: `summable_norm_mayerExpansionTermComplex_succ_of_tail_condition`, `tsum_norm_mayerExpansionTermComplex_succ_le`, and the per-site `tsum_norm_mayerExpansionTermComplex_succ_div_card_le`: `(∑'_n ‖mayerExpansionTermComplex G (n+1) z‖)/|ι| ≤ kpBound (G.maxDegree) ‖z‖`; the `_full` variant `tsum_norm_mayerExpansionTermComplex_div_card_le` (`‖∑'_n mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z‖/|ι| ≤ kpBound (G.maxDegree) ‖z‖`, equal to the succ form since the `n=0` term vanishes, via `norm_tsum_le_tsum_norm`). **Headline** `latticeGraph_kp_tsum_complex_per_site_le_on_ball` (`0 ≤ R`, per-site KP at `2d`/`R`, `z ∈ ball 0 R`): `(∑'_n ‖mayerExpansionTermComplex (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ) (n+1) z‖)/|Λ| ≤ kpBound (2d) R` — **uniform in `Λ` and over the whole ball** (the actual `maxDegree ≤ 2d` KP discharged from the `2d`/`R` ones by `kpRegion_downward_closed`, then dominated by `kpBound (2d) R` via the degree/`r`-monotonicity `kpBound_mono_of_degree_le`/`kpBound_r_mono_of_le`). This is the equicontinuity/normal-family input for the §18.6 Montel/Vitali holomorphic limit. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Per-site complex cluster free energy (holomorphic, bounded)** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.3a: `F_n` def + `DifferentiableOn ℂ`/`AnalyticOnNhd ℂ` + `‖F_n‖ ≤ kpBound (2d) R` on `ball 0 R`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/CubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex.lean`. Packages the per-site complex cluster free energy `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d n z := (∑'_k mayerExpansionTermComplex (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) (cubicBox d n)) k z) / ((cubicBox d n).card : ℂ)` — the cubic-stage-`n` family whose Montel/Vitali limit (D2.3b–d) is the infinite-volume cluster free energy. `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_differentiableOn` (from PR-B `mayerExpansionTermComplex_tsum_differentiableOn_ball` + `DifferentiableOn.div_const`, the `maxDegree ≤ 2d` KP discharged via `kpRegion_downward_closed`), `_analyticOnNhd` (`DifferentiableOn.analyticOnNhd`), and the **uniform-in-`n` bound** `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_norm_le`: `‖cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d n z‖ ≤ kpBound (2d) R` on `ball 0 R` (from PR-D2.2 `latticeGraph_kp_tsum_complex_per_site_le_on_ball` + the `k=0` vanishing `mayerExpansionTermComplex_zero` + `norm_tsum_le_tsum_norm` + the `‖(card:ℂ)‖ = card` cast). The holomorphic, ball-uniformly-bounded family feeding the equicontinuity/Ascoli/Vitali capstone. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Montel compact carrier for the cluster free-energy family** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.3b: equicontinuity + a compact `A ⊆ C(↑(ball 0 R), ℂ)` containing all `F_n`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/CubicMayerClusterMontel.lean`. The Montel/Arzelà–Ascoli step: the holomorphic ball-uniformly-bounded family `F_n` (#4156) is relatively compact in the compact-open topology, so a subsequence will converge locally uniformly (D2.3d). `kpBound_nonneg_of_kpRegion` (`0 ≤ kpBound Δ t` in the KP region), the continuous-map packaging `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplexRestrict d n : C(↑(ball 0 R), ℂ)` (via `AnalyticOnNhd.continuousOn` + `ContinuousOn.restrict`), and `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_equicontinuous` (from `equicontinuous_restrict_of_analyticOnNhd_of_bounded` with `C = kpBound (2d) R`). **Headline** `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_exists_compact_carrier` (`0 < R`, per-site KP at `2d`/`R`): `∃ A : Set C(↑(ball 0 R), ℂ), IsCompact A ∧ ∃ Fc, (∀ n, Fc n ∈ A) ∧ (∀ n z hz, cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d n z = Fc n ⟨z, hz⟩)` — via `isCompact_closureCarrier_compactOpen_complex_of_norm_le_equicontinuous` (`A = toFun ⁻¹' closure (toFun '' (range Fc))`, the index→set equicontinuity through `equicontinuous_range_coe`). The compact carrier feeding the D2.3d subsequence extraction + Vitali limit. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Real-axis convergence of the cluster free energy** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.3c: `F_n(↑t) → ↑(f_∞ t)` for `t ∈ Ioo 0 T`, `T ≤ 1`** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/CubicMayerClusterRealAxis.lean`. The real-axis values that pin the Montel limit (D2.3d, identity theorem). `cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal d t := freeEnergyInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨artanh t, 0, 1⟩ − log 2 − d·log cosh(artanh t)` (the infinite-volume cluster free-energy density, via the activity↔coupling map `tanh(artanh t) = t`, `Real.artanh`). `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_ofReal_eq`: `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d n (↑t) = ↑(polymerFreeEnergy (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) (cubicBox d n)) t / |cubicBox d n|)` (via `Complex.ofReal_tsum` + `mayerExpansionTermComplex_ofReal` + the per-site-KP identity #4152, the actual `maxDegree ≤ 2d` KP discharged from `2d`/`T`). **Headline** `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_tendsto_realAxis` (`t ∈ Ioo 0 T`, `T ≤ 1`): `Tendsto (fun n => cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d n (↑t)) atTop (nhds ↑(cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal d t))`. Proof: the real per-site free energy `polymerFreeEnergy_n(t)/|Λ_n| = freeEnergy_n ⟨artanh t,0,1⟩ − log2 − (edge-density_n)·log cosh(artanh t)` (`freeEnergy_eq_polymerFreeEnergy`, `Real.tanh_artanh`) converges by the cubic free-energy limit (`freeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_tendsto`) and the edge-density limit (#4154), then `Complex.continuous_ofReal`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Whole-sequence Montel/Vitali limit of the cluster free energy** (Issue #4149) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.3d, the crux: holomorphic `F_∞` with whole-sequence locally-uniform convergence** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/CubicMayerClusterLimit.lean`. The Montel/Vitali capstone of the limit construction: the family `F_n` (#4156, holomorphic + ball-uniformly bounded, in a compact carrier #4157) converges as a **whole sequence** to a holomorphic limit `F_∞`, pinned on the real axis by D2.3c. `frequently_ofReal_Ioo_nhdsNE` (the real segment `↑(Ioo 0 T)` accumulates at `↑(T/2)` along `𝓝[≠]`, witnessed by `t0 + δ/(n+2)`), `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_subseq_limit_analyticOnNhd` (any locally-uniform subsequential limit is `AnalyticOnNhd ℂ`, via `vitali_bridge`), `cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_subseq_limit_realAxis` (any subsequential limit `= ↑(f_∞ t)` on `Ioo 0 T`, from D2.3c + `tendsto_nhds_unique`). **Headline** `exists_cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex_limit` (`0 < R`, `0 < T ≤ R`, `T ≤ 1`, per-site KP at `2d`/`R` and `2d`/`T`): `∃ F_∞, DifferentiableOn ℂ F_∞ (ball 0 R) ∧ TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn (cubicMayerClusterFreeEnergyComplex d ·) F_∞ atTop (ball 0 R) ∧ ∀ t ∈ Ioo 0 T, F_∞ ↑t = ↑(cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal d t)`. The whole-sequence convergence uses `IsCompact.tendsto_nhds_of_unique_mapClusterPt`: any cluster point of `(Fc n)` in the compact carrier is a holomorphic subsequential limit (L1) agreeing with `↑(f_∞)` on the real segment (L2), so by the identity theorem (`AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_frequently_eq` + `Metric.isPreconnected_ball` + L0) all cluster points coincide — a unique cluster point ⟹ convergence, transported from compact-open to locally uniform via `continuousMap_tendsto_compactOpen_to_tendstoLocallyUniformlyOn`. The holomorphic infinite-volume cluster free energy on the high-temperature disc — the analytic object for the project §18.6 lattice-analogue coupling-analyticity endpoint (D2.4). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.5–§18.6, pp. 335–340; Friedli–Velenik §5.4. |
| §18.6 analogy | **Infinite-volume free-energy coupling analyticity** (Issue #4149, **CAPSTONE**) | **Conditional / limited-range: PR-D2.4: `AnalyticAt ℝ (β' ↦ freeEnergyInfinite … ⟨J,0,β'⟩) β` at high temperature** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/CubicMayerCouplingAnalyticity.lean`. **The project §18.6 lattice-analogue endpoint (Issue #4149): the infinite-volume ℤ^d Ising free-energy density is real-analytic in the coupling at high temperature**, realised via the cluster expansion. **Scaling** `freeEnergyInfinite_scaling` (general `G`, `Λ`): `freeEnergyInfinite G Λ ⟨J,0,β⟩ = freeEnergyInfinite G Λ ⟨β·J, 0, 1⟩` — at zero field the Boltzmann weight depends only on `β·J` (`boltzmannWeight_scaling`: `exp(-β·(-J·∑edgeSpin)) = exp(-1·(-(βJ)·∑edgeSpin))`, by `interactionEnergy` linearity + `externalFieldEnergy 0 = 0`), lifted through `partitionFunction`/`freeEnergyΛ`/`freeEnergyAlongExhaustion` to the `limsup`. **Bridge** `..._eq_cluster_of_tanh`: `freeEnergyInfinite ⟨c,0,1⟩ = log 2 + d·log cosh(c) + cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal d (tanh c)` (via `Real.artanh_tanh`). **Real-analyticity** `cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal_analyticOnNhd`: `AnalyticOnNhd ℝ (cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal d) (Ioo 0 T)` (the holomorphic `F_∞` of #4159 restricted to the real axis via `AnalyticOnNhd.re_ofReal` + `.congr`). **Headline** `freeEnergyInfinite_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_analyticAt_beta_h_zero` (`0 < βJ`, `tanh βJ < T`, per-site KP at `2d`/`R`,`2d`/`T`, `0 < T ≤ R`, `T ≤ 1`): `AnalyticAt ℝ (fun β' => freeEnergyInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β'⟩) β`, by composing the scaling + bridge with `analyticAt_real_tanh`, `Real.analyticAt_cosh`, `AnalyticAt.log` (`Real.cosh_pos`), `AnalyticAt.comp`, and `cubicInfiniteClusterFreeEnergyReal_analyticOnNhd` at `tanh βJ ∈ Ioo 0 T`; a `J = 1` wrapper `..._analyticAt_beta_unitCoupling`. This is the **project §18.6 lattice-analogue zero-field, high-temperature, cubic-exhaustion infinite-volume coupling-analyticity capstone** — the endpoint of the cluster-expansion chain (the volume-uniform per-site-KP Mayer identity #4150-4152 → free-energy bounds #4153/#4155 → infinite-volume real-axis bound #4154 → the Montel/Vitali holomorphic limit #4156-4159 → this analyticity capstone). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.6, pp. 335ff.; Friedli–Velenik §5.4, §5.7. |
| §18.5 analogy | **KP per-site Mayer bound on the ambient lattice (`ℤ^d`-uniform)** (Issue #3954) | **Conditional / limited-range: induced-Λ / along-exhaustion / `ℤ^d` wrappers + degree-uniform constant** | `ClusterExpansion/MayerTsumPerSiteAmbient.lean`. Lifts the abstract volume-uniform per-site Mayer bound (#4137) to the ambient-lattice induced-graph setting and specialises it to `ℤ^d` with a degree-uniform constant. `kpBound Δ t := ((1 − r)(1 − 4r/(1−r)²))⁻¹` (`r = Δ²·e|t|`). `induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le d Λ : (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ).maxDegree ≤ 2d` (via `inducedLatticeGraph_degree_le` + `maxDegree_le_of_forall_degree_le`). `induced_kp_tsum_per_site_le` / `induced_kp_tsum_per_site_alongExhaustion_le` instantiate #4137 on `inducedGraph G Λ` / `inducedGraph G (Λ.volume n)` (general ambient `V`). The KP constant is **monotone in the degree**: `ratio_mono` (`4r/(1−r)²` increasing in `r`), `kpRegion_downward_closed` (the KP region `r < 1 ∧ 4r/(1−r)² < 1` is downward-closed in `r`), `kpBound_r_mono_of_le` (the per-site constant increasing in `r`, via `inv_anti₀` on the decreasing positive product), and `kpBound_mono_of_degree_le`. **Headline** `latticeGraph_kp_tsum_per_site_le` (`Δ²e|t| < 1`, `4r/(1−r)² < 1` stated at `Δ = 2d`): `(∑'_n |mayerExpansionTerm (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ) (n+1) t|)/|Λ| ≤ kpBound (2d) t` — a per-site Mayer bound **independent of the box `Λ`** (the actual `maxDegree ≤ 2d` discharges the KP hypotheses by downward-closure, then the degree-monotone constant dominates by `kpBound (2d) t`); and `latticeGraph_kp_tsum_per_site_cubicExhaustion_le` gives the same bound uniform over all stages of `cubicExhaustion d`. This closes the §18.5 "Λ-layer + ℤ^d wrappers" tracking item and supplies the volume-uniform analytic input for §18.6. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–§18.5, pp. 332–336; Friedli–Velenik §5.4 (Theorem 5.4). |
| §18.5 analogy | Convergence of the cluster expansion | **Conditional / limited-range: finite-volume/layer `h = 0` + volume-uniform per-site KP convergence + §18.6 high-temperature cubic coupling analyticity** — done at `h = 0` (polymer-family sum sandwich + explicit log-Taylor convergence radius at all volume layers); the volume-uniform per-site Mayer-series convergence is closed via the Kotecký–Preiss tree-graph route (`tsum_abs_mayerExpansionTerm_succ_div_card_le` #4137 + the ambient/`ℤ^d` wrappers `latticeGraph_kp_tsum_per_site_le` #4148); and the §18.6 infinite-volume free-energy analyticity is now realised for the zero-field high-temperature cubic exhaustion (`freeEnergyInfinite_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_analyticAt_beta_h_zero`, #4160, Issue #4149) via the per-site-KP Mayer identity + the Montel/Vitali holomorphic limit | `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_sandwich` (`1 ≤ ∑_Γ ∏ tanh(β·J)^|P| ≤ 2^|E|`) and `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_sandwich_sharp` (`≤ (1+tanh(β·J))^|E|`) under `0 ≤ β·J` (`IsingModel/ClusterExpansion.lean`). **Λ + along-ex + ℤ^d wraps for both sandwich variants** (PR #1572): `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_Λ_sandwich(_sharp)` (`AmbientLattice/Analyticity.lean`), `vdPolymerFamilies_sumAlongExhaustion_sandwich(_sharp)` (`AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/HighTemperature.lean`), `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_Λ_latticeGraph_sandwich(_sharp)` and `vdPolymerFamilies_sumAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_sandwich(_sharp)` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperatureVDPolymer.lean`, carved out of `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperature.lean` in PR #2101). **Ferromagnetic versions of both sandwich variants at all 5 layers** (PR #1574, under `0 ≤ J, 0 < β`): abstract `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_sandwich_ferromagnetic` and `_sandwich_sharp_ferromagnetic`, plus 4 layer wraps for each. The two ℤ^d sharp variants use the abbreviated `_ferro` suffix; other ferromagnetic wrappers retain `_ferromagnetic`. **Strict `freeEnergy` upper bound in convergence regime, all 5 layers** (PR #1575): abstract `freeEnergy_lt_log_two_plus_high_temp_correction` (PR #1527) plus ferromagnetic version, lifted to Λ-direct, along-exhaustion, ℤ^d Λ-direct, and ℤ^d along-exhaustion as `freeEnergyΛ_lt_log_two_plus_high_temp_correction(_ferromagnetic)`, `freeEnergyAlongExhaustion_lt_log_two_plus_high_temp_correction(_ferromagnetic)`, and the corresponding `latticeGraph` versions (with the two ℤ^d ferromagnetic variants using the abbreviated `_ferro` suffix; the four ℤ^d `latticeGraph` `freeEnergyΛ` / `freeEnergyAlongExhaustion` `lt_log_two_plus_high_temp_correction(_ferro)` wrappers now live in `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperatureFreeEnergyCorrection.lean`, carved out of `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperature.lean` in PR #2099; the eight ℤ^d ferromagnetic `polymerFreeEnergy_*_tanh_*_ferro` and `vdPolymerFamilies_sum*_*_ferromagnetic`/`_ferro` wrappers further carved out into `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperatureFerromagnetic.lean` in PR #2100). **`polymerFreeEnergy` regularity at all 4 layers** (PR #1576): `polymerFreeEnergy_{Λ,AlongExhaustion}_{continuousAt,differentiableAt,continuousOn_Ici_zero,differentiableOn_Ici_zero}`. The Λ-layer wrappers remain in `AmbientLattice/Analyticity.lean`; the along-exhaustion and concrete ℤ^d regularity wrappers now live in `AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/PolymerFreeEnergyBounds.lean` and `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/PolymerFreeEnergyBounds.lean` (moved from the legacy modules in PR #1866). 16 thin per-point / per-set wrappers of the abstract `polymerFreeEnergy_continuousAt` / `_differentiableAt` (Step 611) and `_continuousOn_Ici_zero` / `_differentiableOn_Ici_zero`. **`polymerFreeEnergy` β / J real-analyticity at all 4 layers** (PR #1577): `polymerFreeEnergy_{Λ,AlongExhaustion}_tanh_{analyticAt_beta,analyticAt_J,analyticOnNhd_beta_Ici_zero,analyticOnNhd_J_Ici_zero}` plus `latticeGraph` ℤ^d variants. 16 thin wrappers of the abstract `polymerFreeEnergy_tanh_analyticAt_{beta,J}` (Step 613) and `_analyticOnNhd_{beta,J}_Ici_zero` over `Set.Ici 0`. The along-exhaustion and ℤ^d analytic wrappers now live in `AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/PolymerFreeEnergyAnalyticity.lean` and `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/PolymerFreeEnergyAnalyticity.lean` (moved from the legacy modules in PR #1861). **`polymerFreeEnergy` bound family at all 4 layers** (PR #1578): `polymerFreeEnergy_{Λ,AlongExhaustion}_{nonneg_of_nonneg,le_card_log_one_plus_of_nonneg,le_card_mul_of_nonneg,monotoneOn_Ici_zero}` plus `latticeGraph` ℤ^d variants. The four ℤ^d wrappers use the abbreviated `_ferro` suffix in place of `_ferromagnetic` to keep each declaration line within the 100-character linter budget. **Convergence-radius regime (PRs #1517, #1526, #1569)**: under `0 ≤ t` and `(1+t)^|E| < 2`, `polymerFreeEnergy_high_temp_sandwich` packages the 5-statement sandwich `0 ≤ polymerFreeEnergy G t ≤ ε(t) ≤ (1+t)^|E| - 1 < 1` plus `polymerFreeEnergy G t < log 2`; `polymerFreeEnergy_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` gives the explicit log-Taylor `HasSum (fun n => (-1)^n · ε(t)^(n+1) / (n+1)) (polymerFreeEnergy G t)` in the same regime. **Tanh forms** (β·J substituted): `polymerFreeEnergy_tanh_high_temp_sandwich` and `polymerFreeEnergy_tanh_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` under `0 ≤ β·J` and `(1 + tanh(β·J))^|E| < 2`. **Λ-layer wraps** (`IsingModel/AmbientLattice/AnalyticityLambdaSandwich.lean`, narrowed out of `IsingModel/AmbientLattice/Analyticity.lean` in PR #1980): `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_high_temp_sandwich`, `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two`, `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_tanh_high_temp_sandwich`, and `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_tanh_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two`. **Along-exhaustion wraps**: `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_high_temp_sandwich` and `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` (`IsingModel/AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/HighTemperature.lean`); `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_tanh_high_temp_sandwich` and `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_tanh_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` (`IsingModel/AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/HighTemperatureTanh.lean`) (4 theorems, per-stage `n`). **ℤ^d Λ-direct wraps** (`IsingModel/Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperature.lean`): `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_latticeGraph_high_temp_sandwich`, `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_latticeGraph_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two`, `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_latticeGraph_tanh_high_temp_sandwich`, and `polymerFreeEnergy_Λ_latticeGraph_tanh_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` (4 theorems on `latticeGraph d × Finset (Fin d → ℤ)`). **ℤ^d along-exhaustion wraps** (`IsingModel/Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperatureAlongEx.lean`): `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_high_temp_sandwich`, `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two`, `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_tanh_high_temp_sandwich`, and `polymerFreeEnergyAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_tanh_hasSum_via_log_of_pow_lt_two` (4 theorems on `latticeGraph d × Exhaustion (Fin d → ℤ)`). The subsequent Penrose tree-graph, per-site Kotecký–Preiss, and degree-uniform radius rows implement the sharper polymer-density/KP estimates and the radius-of-convergence control used by the §18.5–§18.6 capstones; this chain no longer leaves those internal estimates deferred. The along-exhaustion and concrete high-temperature sandwich, `HasSum`, polymer-family sandwich, and free-energy correction wrappers now live in `AmbientLattice/SpecialCases/HighTemperature.lean` and `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/HighTemperature.lean` (moved from the legacy modules in PR #1864); the Λ-layer wrappers remain in `IsingModel/AmbientLattice/Analyticity.lean`. (Steps 549-554; PRs #1517, #1526, #1569; Issue #1344.) |

The `h` direction does not carry the `_general_h` suffix because the abstract theorem name uses `_h` directly. **Joint analyticity** (PR #1531): `freeEnergy_analyticAt_joint` — `f` is `AnalyticAt ℝ` jointly in `(β, J, h)` at every point. **Joint AnalyticOnNhd** (PR #1532): `freeEnergy_analyticOnNhd_joint` over `Set.univ`. **Joint Continuous + Differentiable** (PR #1533): `freeEnergy_continuous_joint`, `freeEnergy_differentiable_joint`. **Λ-layer joint analyticity** (PR #1535): introduces `IsingModel/AmbientLattice/Analyticity.lean` and lifts the joint analyticity / Continuous / Differentiable hierarchy to `freeEnergyΛ`. **Correlation joint analyticity** (PRs #1536, #1537): `correlation_analyticAt_joint` and the full regularity hierarchy (Continuous / Differentiable / AnalyticAt / AnalyticOnNhd) at the abstract and Λ layers. **`gibbsExpectation_analyticAt_joint`** (PR #1539): joint analyticity of `gibbsExpectation` for an arbitrary observable `F : Configuration → ℝ`. **`partitionFunction` Continuous + Differentiable per-direction at general h** (PR #1540) and Λ-layer wrappers (PR #1541). **`partitionFunction` h_zero regularity at all 4 layers** (PR #1608): adds `partitionFunctionΛ_{continuous,differentiable,analyticAt,analyticOnNhd}_{beta,J}_h_zero`, `partitionFunctionAlongExhaustion_{continuous,differentiable,analyticAt,analyticOnNhd}_{beta,J}_h_zero` plus `latticeGraph` ℤ^d variants. 32 thin wrappers (8 abstracts × 4 layers) lifting the polymer-expansion-derived h_zero regularity (`Continuous` / `Differentiable ℝ` / `AnalyticAt ℝ` / `AnalyticOnNhd ℝ _ Set.univ`) of `Z(J, 0, β)` in β / J to all 4 volume layers; ≠ 0`) and `ContinuousAt.eventually_ne` to give: the `tanh`-substituted polymer-family sum is non-zero in a complex neighborhood of `β = 0`. This makes the second-moment / Cauchy-style route an equally valid entry point to the same CE-route bundle. **One-step Cauchy Q-circle → final-capstone wrappers**: `lemma_17_5_2_upper_bound_of_Q_and_circle` and `lemma_17_5_2_sandwich_of_Q_and_circle` mirror the CE-route Props one-step wrappers, allowing a single Q-circle assembler (with ne-zero parts assembled from Q-bounds) to deliver the named upper-bound predicate or the displayed two-sided sandwich directly. **Unconditional per-fixed-volume Cauchy Q-bound**: `hamiltonian_sq_le` (`ComplexAnalyticity/SecondMomentBounds.lean`) gives the trivial pointwise bound `H(σ; G, p)² ≤ (|J|·|E| + |h|·|ι|)²` via `hamiltonian_abs_le`; `second_moment_bound_trivial` upgrades to the Boltzmann-weighted form `∑_σ exp(-β.re · H) · H² ≤ (|J|·|E| + |h|·|ι|)² · Z_ℝ`; `partitionFunctionComplex_ne_zero_of_im_lt_h_zero` then composes with PR #3048 to give an unconditional per-fixed-volume `Z_ℂ ≠ 0` on a complex disc of explicit radius `√2 / (|J|·|E|)` at `h = 0`. `partitionFunctionComplex_ne_zero_on_closedBall_h_zero_at_zero` (same file) packages the explicit form: for `r * (|J|·|E|) < √2`, `Z_ℂ ≠ 0` on the entire `Metric.closedBall (0:ℂ) r` — the exact `hZk`/`hZk1` shape of #3032 with explicit unconditional radius. ≤ p.β·p.J·(2·d·(2(k+1)+1)^d·cf^{(k+1−R)/(r₀+2)})` form (using ferromagnetic monotonicity `correlationAlongExhaustion_monotone` to upgrade the one-sided cubic bound). **Cubic abs in direct (liftFinset {r, s} _)` shape required by the `h_real_inc` slots of the poly-geometric CE-route bundle constructors (via `correlationAlongExhaustion_of_subset` to unfold the along-exhaustion form). Direct input for the cubic + Ferromagnetic + h=0 + contraction-factor-smallness + separation context. **High-temperature simplification**: `abs_correlation_inducedGraph_cubic_succ_sub_le_poly_pow_high_temp` absorbs the `β · J · 2 · d` prefactor under the standard high-temperature condition `β · J · 2 · d ≤ 1`, leaving the clean `(2k+3)^d · cf^{(k+1-R)/(r₀+2)}` form — directly compatible with the `R_inc_seq k := (2k+3)^d · ratio^k` shape required by the poly-geometric CE-route bundle constructors. **Adjacent vertex in next cubicBox**: `cubicBox_succ_of_latticeGraph_adj` (same file) gives `r ∈ cubicBox d R ∧ (latticeGraph d).Adj r y → y ∈ cubicBox d (R + 1)` — combinatorial building block for the separation hypothesis discharge. **Separation hypothesis discharge from `R + 1 ≤ k`**: `not_sym2_mem_straddle_of_cubicBox_R_succ_le_k` (single-vertex) and `hsep_of_cubicBox_R_succ_le_k` (pair) automatically discharge the `hsep` separation hypothesis required by the cubic per-stage increment bounds, given only `r, s ∈ cubicBox d R` and `R + 1 ≤ k`. Proof: any neighbor of `r` (or `s`) lies in `cubicBox d (R + 1) ⊆ cubicBox d k` (via the previous building block), and `r ∈ cubicBox d R ⊆ cubicBox d k`, so both endpoints of any incident edge are in `cubicBox d k` — contradicting `straddle_fresh_vertex`. Removes the final combinatorial input from the cubic CE-route plumbing. **Floor-power → geometric bound**: `cf_pow_natDiv_le_geometric` (same file) gives, for `0 < cf < 1` and `m ≥ 1`, the closed-form geometric upper bound `cf^⌊n/m⌋ ≤ (1/cf) · ρ^n` where `ρ := cf^(1/m)` (real power), with `0 < ρ < 1`. Step A of the cubic CE-route plumbing: converts the cubic real-axis floor-decay exponent `cf^⌊(k+1-R)/(r₀+2)⌋` to the geometric `M_R · ρ_R^k` shape required by the poly-geometric CE-route bundle constructors. **Cubic abs in clean geometric high-temperature form** (Steps A+B composition): `abs_correlation_inducedGraph_cubic_succ_sub_le_geometric_high_temp` combines the high-temperature simplification (#3116), the auto-discharged `hsep` (#3118), and the floor → geometric bound (#3119) to produce the cubic real-axis abs increment in the clean form `|c_k − c_{k+1}| ≤ (1/cf) · (2k+3)^d · ρ_R^{k+1−R}` with explicit `ρ_R := cf^{1/(r₀+2)} ∈ (0, 1)`.
